# 📘 Part 16: Understanding **Helper Classes** in ASP.NET Core MVC

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab tak hum MVC ke kaun-kaun se basics dekh chuke hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, humne ye sab dekh liya:

* **Controller** kaise banate hain
* **View** kaise banate hain
* **Model** kaise banate hain
* Controller ↔ View ke beech **data passing**
* View → Controller aur Controller → View data flow

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab next topic kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, next topic hai **HTML Helper Classes** in ASP.NET Core MVC.

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper Classes kya hoti hain?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: HTML Helper Classes ka main kaam kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
**HTML Helper Classes ka kaam hai HTML code generate karna**, bina manually HTML likhe.

👉 Matlab:

* Aap **C# / Razor code** likhte ho
* Helper class **runtime par HTML generate** kar deti hai

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Runtime par HTML generate hone ka matlab?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Matlab **HTML pehle se page me likha hua nahi hota**.
Jab:

* User request karta hai
* View load hota hai

👉 Tab **HTML Helper** automatically HTML bana deta hai.

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper use kyun karte hain?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Hum direct HTML likh sakte hain, fir helper kyun?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, helper use karne ke **bahut fayde** hain:

1. **Manual HTML kam likhna**
2. **Code short & clean** ho jata hai
3. **Typing mistakes (typos)** kam hoti hain
4. **Model ke saath easily bind** hota hai
5. **Validation** easy ho jata hai
6. **Strongly typed coding** possible hoti hai

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Example de sakte ho?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

👉 Normal HTML:

```html
<input type="text" name="UserName" />
```

👉 HTML Helper se:

```csharp
@Html.TextBox("UserName")
```

✔ Same output
✔ Kam code
✔ Readable

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper kya-kya generate kar sakta hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Kaun-kaun se controls HTML Helper bana sakta hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
HTML Helper se hum ye sab bana sakte hain:

* **TextBox**
* **PasswordBox**
* **TextArea**
* **CheckBox**
* **RadioButton**
* **DropDownList**
* **Form**
* **Labels**

---

## 🔹 Model ke saath HTML Helper ka role

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Model ke saath helper ka kya fayda hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, ye **sabse bada advantage** hai.

* Agar **Model me data hai** → Form auto-fill ho jata hai
* Agar **Form submit hota hai** → Data auto Model me bind ho jata hai

👉 Isko bolte hain **Model Binding**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Normal HTML me ye kaam kaise hota?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Normal HTML me:

* Value manually set karni padti
* JavaScript likhni padti
* Validation manually karni padti

👉 HTML Helper + Model se ye sab **automatic** ho jata hai.

---

## 🔹 Razor View clean kyun hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Helper use karne se Razor View clean kaise hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Kyuki:

* HTML tags kam ho jate hain
* Zyada tar **C# / Razor syntax** hota hai
* Code readable & maintainable hota hai

👉 Clean View = Less Bugs 👍

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper ke types

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: ASP.NET Core MVC me HTML Helper ke types kaun se hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **3 types** hote hain:

---

### 1️⃣ **Standard HTML Helpers**

* Sirf **HTML control generate** karte hain
* **Model se bind nahi hote**

📌 Example:

```csharp
@Html.TextBox("UserName")
```

---

### 2️⃣ **Strongly Typed HTML Helpers**

* **Model ke saath directly linked** hote hain
* Model ka data automatically show hota hai
* Validation easy hoti hai

📌 Example:

```csharp
@Html.TextBoxFor(m => m.UserName)
```

---

### 3️⃣ **Custom HTML Helpers**

* Jab **built-in helper available na ho**
* Developer **khud helper banata hai**

📌 Example use-case:

* Custom image tag
* Reusable complex UI block

👉 Isko bolte hain **Custom Helper Class**

---

## 🔹 Custom Helper ka idea

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Custom Helper kab banate hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Jab:

* Koi HTML element baar-baar use ho raha ho
* Built-in helper available na ho
* Code reuse chahiye

👉 Tab hum **Custom HTML Helper** banate hain.

---

## 🔹 Important Summary (Exam / Interview)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: One-line summary batao

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* **HTML Helper = C# methods to generate HTML**
* **Runtime par HTML generate hota hai**
* **Manual HTML likhne ki need kam hoti hai**
* **Model binding + validation easy hoti hai**
* **3 types: Standard, Strongly Typed, Custom**

---

## ✅ Final Notes

**👨‍🏫 Teacher:**
Aage ke videos me hum:

* ✔ Standard Helper practically
* ✔ Strongly Typed Helper practically
* ✔ Custom Helper banana

step-by-step dekhenge.

---

Agar chaho to next:

* 👉 **Standard HTML Helper with example**
* 👉 **Strongly Typed Helper vs Normal Helper**
* 👉 **Interview Q&A on Helper Classes**

Bas batao 👍

# 📘 Part 17: Using **Standard HTML Helper** in ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## 🔹 Recap

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Pichhle video me humne kya padha tha?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, pichhle topic me humne **HTML Helper Classes ka introduction** dekha tha.

* **HTML Helpers** ka kaam → **HTML code generate karna**
* Bina manually HTML likhe
* Sirf **Razor (C#) code** likhne se HTML ban jaata hai

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper ka main fayda

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: HTML Helper use kyun karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, kyunki jab:

* HTML + C# mix likhte hain
* Code **messy** ho jaata hai

HTML Helper se:

* Code **clean** hota hai
* Ya to zyada **C#** hota hai
* Ya zyada **HTML**, mix kam hota hai

---

## 🔹 HTML Helpers ke types (recap)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: HTML Helper kitne type ke hote hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **3 types** hote hain:

1. **Standard HTML Helper** ✅ (aaj ka topic)
2. **Strongly Typed HTML Helper**
3. **Custom HTML Helper**

---

## 🔹 Standard HTML Helper kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Standard HTML Helper ka matlab?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Standard HTML Helpers** use hote hain:

* **Common HTML controls generate karne ke liye**
* Model binding nahi hoti (simple controls)

### 🔧 Ye kya-kya generate kar sakta hai?

* **Form**
* **Label**
* **TextBox**
* **Password**
* **TextArea**
* **CheckBox**
* **RadioButton**
* **DropDownList**
* **ActionLink**

---

## 🔹 HTML Helper ka syntax

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: HTML Helper likhne ka syntax kya hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **HTML Helper hamesha `@Html` se start hota hai**:

```csharp
@Html.Label()
@Html.TextBox()
@Html.Password()
```

👉 `Html` ek **object** hai
👉 Dot (`.`) lagane par uske **methods** milte hain

---

## 🔹 Project setup

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Practical kaise start karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir:

1. New **ASP.NET Core MVC project** banao
2. Default **HomeController** aur **Index.cshtml** mil jaata hai
3. Hum **Index.cshtml** me hi kaam karenge

---

## 🔹 Form create using Standard HTML Helper

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Form kaise banate hain HTML Helper se?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, form ke liye hum use karte hain:

### ✅ **Html.BeginForm()**

```csharp
@using (Html.BeginForm("Validate", "Home", FormMethod.Post))
{
    // form content
}
```

👉 Ye automatically generate karega:

```html
<form action="/Home/Validate" method="post">
```

---

## 🔹 Label generate karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Label kaise banate hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.Label("Enter User ID")
```

➡ Generates:

```html
<label>Enter User ID</label>
```

---

## 🔹 TextBox generate karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: TextBox kaise banega?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.TextBox("UserName")
```

➡ Generates:

```html
<input type="text" name="UserName" />
```

---

## 🔹 Password box generate karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Password field?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.Password("Password")
```

➡ Generates:

```html
<input type="password" name="Password" />
```

---

## 🔹 Complete Login Form (Example)

### 📌 Razor Code (Index.cshtml)

```csharp
@using (Html.BeginForm("Validate", "Home", FormMethod.Post))
{
    <div>
        @Html.Label("Enter User ID")
        @Html.TextBox("UserName")
    </div>

    <div>
        @Html.Label("Enter Password")
        @Html.Password("Password")
    </div>

    <div>
        <input type="submit" value="Login" />
    </div>
}
```

---

## 🔹 Important Point ⚠️

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Submit button ke liye helper kyun use nahi hua?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, kyunki:

* **Har HTML element ke liye helper available nahi hota**
* Button ke liye hum normal HTML likhte hain

```html
<input type="submit" value="Login" />
```

---

## 🔹 Attributes add karna (class, placeholder, etc.)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar CSS class ya placeholder chahiye ho to?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **anonymous object** use karte hain:

```csharp
@Html.TextBox(
    "UserName",
    "",
    new { @class = "form-control", placeholder = "Enter username" }
)
```

➡ Generates:

```html
<input type="text"
       name="UserName"
       class="form-control"
       placeholder="Enter username" />
```

---

## 🔹 Bootstrap ke saath use

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Bootstrap class kaise lagate hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, same tarike se:

* Label → `form-label`
* TextBox / Password → `form-control`

```csharp
@Html.Label("User Name", new { @class = "form-label" })
@Html.TextBox("UserName", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
```

---

## 🔹 Layout & Styling ka note

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: HTML Helper layout bhi set karta hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
❌ Nahi sir

* HTML Helper **sirf HTML generate karta hai**
* **Alignment / spacing / design** ke liye:

  * CSS
  * Bootstrap
  * Div / Row / Column use karna padta hai

---

## 🔹 View Source se kya samjha?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Page Source dekhne se kya pata chala?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir:

* Razor code → **runtime pe HTML me convert** ho gaya
* Browser ko sirf **pure HTML** milta hai
* Helper ka kaam → **automatic HTML generation**

---

## 🔹 Summary (Exam / Interview Ready)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Short summary do

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* **Standard HTML Helper**

  * `@Html.Label()`
  * `@Html.TextBox()`
  * `@Html.Password()`
* Clean & readable code
* No model binding
* Form & common controls ke liye best
* Attributes ke liye `new { }` use hota hai
* Styling ke liye CSS / Bootstrap zaroori

# 📘 Part 18: Creating **Signup Form** using **HTML Helper** in ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## 🔹 Recap

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Pichhle video me humne kya dekha tha?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, pichhle topic me humne **Standard HTML Helper** ka use dekha tha:

* **Form**
* **Label**
* **TextBox**
* **Password Box**

Aur seekha tha ki **bina manually HTML likhe**, helpers se HTML generate hota hai.

---

## 🔹 Aaj ka goal

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Aaj hum kya banane wale hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, aaj hum **complete Signup Form** banayenge jisme ye sab fields honge:

* **TextBox**
* **Password Box**
* **Radio Button**
* **Check Box**
* **TextArea**
* **DropDown List**
* **Submit / Reset Button**
* **Action Link (Login link)**

👉 Sab kuch **Standard HTML Helper** se.

---

## 🔹 Project setup

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Project kaise start karenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

1. **New ASP.NET Core MVC Project**
2. Project name: `StandardHtmlHelper`
3. **HomeController → Index.cshtml** ko edit karenge

---

## 🔹 Responsive layout (Bootstrap use)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Form ko responsive kaise banaya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, humne **Bootstrap Grid System** use kiya:

```html
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row">
        <div class="col-lg-4 col-md-6 col-sm-12">
```

### 📱 Meaning:

* **LG (Desktop)** → 1/3 screen
* **MD (Tablet)** → Half screen
* **SM (Mobile)** → Full screen

---

## 🔹 Form start (HTML Helper)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Form kaise start kiya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@using (Html.BeginForm("SaveData", "Home", FormMethod.Post))
{
    // form content
}
```

➡ Automatically generate karega:

```html
<form method="post" action="/Home/SaveData">
```

---

## 🔹 Name TextBox

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Name field kaise banaya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.Label("nm", "Enter Your Name")
@Html.TextBox("nm", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
```

🔹 **Label ka `for` attribute** TextBox ke `id` se match hota hai
➡ Label pe click karne se cursor TextBox me aata hai

---

## 🔹 Father’s Name TextBox

```csharp
@Html.Label("fnm", "Enter Father Name")
@Html.TextBox("fnm", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
```

---

## 🔹 Password & Retype Password

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Password field kaise banaya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.Label("pass", "Enter Password")
@Html.Password("pass", "", new { @class = "form-control" })

@Html.Label("rpass", "Retype Password")
@Html.Password("rpass", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
```

➡ `type="password"` automatically generate hota hai

---

## 🔹 Gender (Radio Buttons)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Gender ke liye Radio Button kyun?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, jab **multiple options me se sirf ek select karna ho**, tab **Radio Button** use hota hai.

```csharp
@Html.Label("gen", "Gender")

@Html.RadioButton("gen", "Male", true) Male
@Html.RadioButton("gen", "Female", false) Female
@Html.RadioButton("gen", "Other", false) Other
```

### 🔑 Important:

* **Name same hona chahiye** (`gen`)
* Tabhi ek select hone par baaki deselect honge
* **Value wahi jayegi** jo select hui

---

## 🔹 Course (DropDown List)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: DropDown kaise banaya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### Step 1️⃣: List create karo

```csharp
@{
    List<string> crs = new List<string>()
    {
        "DCA", "MCA", "PGDCA"
    };
}
```

### Step 2️⃣: DropDownList helper

```csharp
@Html.DropDownList(
    "course",
    new SelectList(crs),
    "Select Course",
    new { @class = "form-control" }
)
```

➡ `"Select Course"` ek **placeholder** ki tarah kaam karta hai

---

## 🔹 Hobbies (Check Boxes)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: CheckBox kab use karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, jab **multiple options select ho sakte hain**, tab **CheckBox** use hota hai.

```csharp
@Html.Label("hobbies", "Select Hobbies")

@Html.CheckBox("Cricket") Cricket
@Html.CheckBox("Music") Music
@Html.CheckBox("Reading") Reading
```

➡ Multiple checkboxes **true/false** ke form me controller ko value bhejte hain

---

## 🔹 Address (TextArea)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Address ke liye TextBox kyun nahi?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, Address **multi-line** hota hai, isliye **TextArea** use karte hain.

```csharp
@Html.Label("addr", "Enter Address")
@Html.TextArea("addr", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
```

---

## 🔹 Buttons (Submit & Reset)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Button ke liye helper kyun use nahi hua?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Button ke liye HTML Helper nahi hota**, isliye normal HTML likhte hain:

```html
<input type="submit" value="Register Me" class="btn btn-success" />
<input type="reset" value="Reset" class="btn btn-danger" />
```

---

## 🔹 Action Link (Login link)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Hyperlink kaise banaya?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.ActionLink("Click here to Login", "Login", "Home")
```

➡ Generates:

```html
<a href="/Home/Login">Click here to Login</a>
```

---

## 🔹 Hidden Field (Concept)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Hidden field kya hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir:

* **User ko dikhai nahi deta**
* But **form submit hone par value controller ko jaati hai**
* Mostly **security / session / key validation** ke liye use hota hai

Example:

```csharp
@Html.Hidden("secretKey", "ABC123")
```

---

## 🔹 Form submit hone par data kaise check kare?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Kaise pata chale data aa raha hai ya nahi?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

1. Controller me action method banao:

```csharp
public IActionResult SaveData(IFormCollection f)
{
    return View();
}
```

2. **Breakpoint lagao**
3. Debug mode me `f` ke andar:

   * Name
   * Password
   * Gender
   * Course
   * Hobbies
   * Address
     sab values dikh jaati hain

➡ Isse debugging easy ho jaati hai

---

## 🔹 Important Debugging Tip ⭐

* **Breakpoint** = red dot
* Program execution wahi ruk jaata hai
* Variables ka value inspect kar sakte ho
* Dubara click → breakpoint remove

---

## 🔹 Summary (Exam / Interview Ready)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Short summary bolo

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* **Standard HTML Helper** se complete **Signup Form** bana sakte hain
* Common helpers:

  * `Label`
  * `TextBox`
  * `Password`
  * `RadioButton`
  * `CheckBox`
  * `TextArea`
  * `DropDownList`
  * `ActionLink`
* Buttons manually HTML se
* Styling → **Bootstrap**
* Debugging → **IFormCollection + Breakpoint**

# 📘 Part 19: **Using Strongly Typed HTML Helpers**

ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## 🔹 Recap

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab tak hum HTML Helpers ke kaun-kaun se types dekh chuke hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, ab tak humne ye dekha hai:

1. **Standard HTML Helpers**
2. **Strongly Typed HTML Helpers**
3. **Custom HTML Helpers**

Aur ek aur type hota hai:
4. **Templated Helpers**
(jisme **EditorFor** aur **DisplayFor** aata hai – wo next topic hai)

---

## 🔹 Strongly Typed HTML Helper kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Strongly Typed Helper ka main concept kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Strongly Typed HTML Helper** bhi wahi kaam karta hai jo Standard Helper karta hai:

* **Label**
* **TextBox**
* **Password**
* **TextArea**
* **DropDown**
* **RadioButton**
* **CheckBox**

❗ **BUT main difference ye hai**:

👉 **Strongly Typed Helper model ke saath linked hota hai**

---

## 🔹 Standard vs Strongly Typed (Core Difference)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Dono me exact difference batao

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

| Standard Helper              | Strongly Typed Helper                      |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| Model se link nahi hota      | **Model se directly linked hota hai**      |
| Name manually dena padta hai | **Property name model se aata hai**        |
| Data manually extract karna  | **Model automatically fill hota hai**      |
| Pre-filled data nahi aata    | **Pre-filled data automatically aata hai** |

👉 **Edit form** (Update screen) me strongly typed helper best hota hai.

---

## 🔹 Strongly Typed Helper ka biggest fayda

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Iska sabse bada benefit kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

1. **Form submit karte hi data Model me aa jata hai**
2. Controller ko directly **Model object milta hai**
3. Agar View ko Model ke saath call kiya:

   * Form **pre-filled** hota hai
4. **Less error**, **clean code**

---

## 🔹 Step 1: Model banana (Mandatory)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Strongly Typed Helper use karne ke liye sabse pehle kya chahiye?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Model class mandatory hoti hai**.

### 📌 Example: `Login.cs` (Model)

```csharp
public class Login
{
    public string UserName { get; set; }
    public string Password { get; set; }
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 2: Controller structure samajhna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Kya hamesha HomeController hi use hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Nahi sir ❌

Best practice ye hai:

* **Entity wise controller**
* Jaise:

  * Student → `StudentController`
  * Login → `LoginController`

---

## 🔹 Step 3: Home page se dusre controller ko call karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Home page se Login form kaise open karenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### 📌 `Home/Index.cshtml`

```html
<a href="/Student/Index">Show Login Form</a>
```

➡ Ye `StudentController` ke `Index()` action ko call karega.

---

## 🔹 Step 4: StudentController banana

### 📌 `StudentController.cs`

```csharp
public class StudentController : Controller
{
    public IActionResult Index()
    {
        Login l = new Login
        {
            UserName = "admin",
            Password = "123"
        };

        return View(l);
    }
}
```

👉 Yahan:

* Model ka **object bana ke**
* **View ko pass** kiya ja raha hai
  ➡ Isliye form **pre-filled** aayega

---

## 🔹 Step 5: Strongly Typed View banana

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: View ko model ke saath kaise bind karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### 📌 View ke top me:

```csharp
@model StrongTypeDemo.Models.Login
```

➡ Ab ye view **strongly typed** ho gaya.

---

## 🔹 Step 6: Strongly Typed HTML Helpers use karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Label aur TextBox ka syntax kya hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **`For` keyword** use hota hai.

### 📌 Label + TextBox

```csharp
@Html.LabelFor(x => x.UserName)
@Html.TextBoxFor(x => x.UserName)
```

➡ `x` yahan **model ka object** hai
➡ `UserName` = model ki property

---

### 📌 Password field

```csharp
@Html.LabelFor(x => x.Password)
@Html.PasswordFor(x => x.Password)
```

🔴 **Note:**
Password field **pre-filled value show nahi karta** (security reason)

---

## 🔹 Lambda Expression samjho (Important ⭐)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: `x => x.UserName` ka matlab kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* `x` → Model ka object
* `x.UserName` → Model ki property
* Ye syntax bolta hai:
  👉 “Is control ko is property se bind karo”

Isko bolte hain **Lambda Expression**

---

## 🔹 Default Label text kaise decide hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Label me kaunsa text aata hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
By default:

* **Property ka naam** hi label ban jata hai

  * `UserName` → UserName
  * `Password` → Password

---

## 🔹 Label text customize kaise kare?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar label text change karna ho?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Data Annotation Attribute** use karte hain.

### 📌 Model me change:

```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

public class Login
{
    [Display(Name = "User Name")]
    public string UserName { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Password")]
    public string Password { get; set; }
}
```

➡ Ab label me **User Name** aur **Password** show hoga

---

## 🔹 Step 7: Form submit karna (Model ke saath)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Submit pe data kahan jayega?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **directly model object ke form me** controller ko milega.

### 📌 Controller method

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Login(Login l)
{
    return Content(l.UserName + " " + l.Password);
}
```

➡ Data **automatically bind** ho gaya
➡ No `Request.Form`, no `IFormCollection`

---

## 🔹 Form kaise bind hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Binding kaise ho rahi hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* View ke controls **model properties se linked**
* Submit pe:

  * Model ka object ban jata hai
  * Values auto fill ho jati hain
* Controller ko **ready-to-use object** milta hai

---

## 🔹 Strongly Typed Helper kahan use hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Real projects me kahan use karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* **Login Form**
* **Registration Form**
* **Edit Profile**
* **Update Records**
* **CRUD with Database**
* **Entity Framework ke saath**

👉 **Database + MVC = Strongly Typed Helpers mandatory**

---

## 🔹 Strongly Typed Helper ka syntax summary

```csharp
@Html.LabelFor(x => x.Property)
@Html.TextBoxFor(x => x.Property)
@Html.PasswordFor(x => x.Property)
@Html.TextAreaFor(x => x.Property)
@Html.CheckBoxFor(x => x.Property)
@Html.RadioButtonFor(x => x.Property, "Value")
@Html.DropDownListFor(x => x.Property, list)
```

---

## 🔹 Next Topic Preview 🚀

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Next video me kya aayega?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, next topic hoga:

👉 **Templated HTML Helpers**

* **EditorFor**
* **DisplayFor**

🔹 Jo **data type ke according automatically control choose** karte hain

---

## ✅ Final One-Line Summary

**Strongly Typed HTML Helpers** =
👉 *Model-linked controls* + *automatic binding* + *clean & professional MVC code*

---

Agar chaho to main:

* **Strongly Typed Signup Form**
* **Standard vs Strong vs Template (comparison table)**
* **Interview Q&A (HTML Helpers + MVC)**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

# 📘 Part 20: **Using Templated HTML Helpers**

ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## 🔹 Recap (Pichhle topics ka short summary)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab tak hum kaun-kaun se HTML Helpers dekh chuke hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, ab tak humne ye dekha hai:

1. **Standard HTML Helpers**
2. **Strongly Typed HTML Helpers**

Aur aaj ka naya topic hai:
3. **Templated HTML Helpers**

---

## 🔹 Templated HTML Helper kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Templated HTML Helper ka main idea kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Templated HTML Helper** ka sabse bada fayda ye hai ki:

👉 Hume **alag-alag controls ke liye alag-alag methods yaad rakhne ki zarurat nahi hoti**

Bas **ek hi helper** use karte hain, aur:

* Wo **model ki property ke data type** ke basis par
* **Automatically decide** karta hai
  ki kaunsa control banana hai

---

## 🔹 Standard / Strongly Typed vs Templated

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Templated helper kyun better mana jata hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

| Type                      | Kya problem                                                       |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Standard Helper**       | TextBox, PasswordBox, DropDown sab alag-alag yaad rakhna          |
| **Strongly Typed Helper** | Fir bhi alag-alag helpers use karne padte                         |
| ✅ **Templated Helper**    | **Sirf Editor / EditorFor** use karo, baaki kaam framework karega |

---

## 🔹 Templated HTML Helpers ke types

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Templated helpers kaun-kaun se hote hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, mainly do helpers hote hain:

1. **Editor**
2. **EditorFor**

Aur label ke liye:

* **Display**
* **DisplayFor**

---

## 🔹 Editor vs EditorFor

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Editor aur EditorFor me difference kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

| Helper        | Type                             |
| ------------- | -------------------------------- |
| **Editor**    | Normal / loosely typed           |
| **EditorFor** | **Strongly typed (Model bound)** |

👉 Real projects me **EditorFor** zyada use hota hai.

---

## 🔹 Step 1: Model banana (Mandatory)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Templated helper ke liye sabse pehle kya chahiye?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Model class banana compulsory hai**, kyunki control **data type ke basis par** banta hai.

### 📌 Example: `Student.cs`

```csharp
public class Student
{
    public int Roll { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public DateTime DOB { get; set; }
    public bool IsDisabled { get; set; }
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 2: Controller se Model pass karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: View ko model kaise milega?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, controller se model ka object pass karna padta hai.

### 📌 Controller code

```csharp
public IActionResult Index()
{
    Student s = new Student();
    return View(s);
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 3: View ko Strongly Typed banana

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: View me model kaise receive karte hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### 📌 View ke top me:

```csharp
@model TemplatedHelperDemo.Models.Student
```

➡ Ab ye view **strongly typed** ho gaya.

---

## 🔹 Step 4: Editor helper use karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab form controls kaise banayenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, ab hume **TextBox / PasswordBox / CheckBox likhne ki zarurat nahi**.

### 📌 Simple example:

```csharp
@Html.Editor("Roll")
<br />
@Html.Editor("Name")
<br />
@Html.Editor("DOB")
<br />
@Html.Editor("IsDisabled")
```

---

## 🔹 Output ka magic ✨

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ye kaunsa control kaise decide karta hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **property ke data type** ke basis par:

| Property Type | Generated Control          |
| ------------- | -------------------------- |
| `int`         | **Number input**           |
| `string`      | **TextBox**                |
| `DateTime`    | **Date / DateTime picker** |
| `bool`        | **CheckBox**               |

👉 Humne **kahin bhi explicitly control type nahi likha**, phir bhi sab sahi bana.

---

## 🔹 Label kahan se aayega?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Editor se label kyun nahi aata?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, label ke liye alag helper hota hai:

👉 **Display / DisplayFor**

---

## 🔹 DisplayFor use karna

### 📌 Example:

```csharp
@Html.DisplayFor(x => x.Roll)
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.Roll)

@Html.DisplayFor(x => x.Name)
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.Name)
```

---

## 🔹 Default label ka naam kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Label me kaunsa text show hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
By default:
👉 **Property ka naam hi label ban jata hai**

* `Roll` → Roll
* `Name` → Name
* `DOB` → DOB

---

## 🔹 Label text customize kaise kare?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar label ko readable banana ho?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Data Annotation – Display attribute** use karte hain.

### 📌 Model me change:

```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

public class Student
{
    [Display(Name = "Roll Number")]
    public int Roll { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Student Name")]
    public string Name { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Date of Birth")]
    public DateTime DOB { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Any Disability")]
    public bool IsDisabled { get; set; }
}
```

➡ Ab labels user-friendly ho jayenge.

---

## 🔹 Data type change = Control change

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar DateTime ko sirf Date kar dein to?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, jaise hi data type change hota hai:

* **Control automatically change ho jata hai**
* Ye hi **Templated helper ka real power** hai

---

## 🔹 EditorFor (Strongly Typed Templated Helper)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: EditorFor ka syntax kya hota hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.Roll)
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.Name)
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.DOB)
@Html.EditorFor(x => x.IsDisabled)
```

👉 Yahan:

* `x` = Model object
* `x.Property` = Model property

---

## 🔹 Button ka kya?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Submit button ke liye helper kyun nahi?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, **Button ke liye built-in helper nahi hota**, isliye HTML likhna padta hai.

### 📌 Example:

```html
<input type="submit" value="Save" class="btn btn-success" />
```

---

## 🔹 ActionLink (Hyperlink helper)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Hyperlink kaise banate hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### 📌 Syntax:

```csharp
@Html.ActionLink("Forgot Password", "ForgotPass", "Home")
```

👉 Ye:

* Text dikhata hai
* Click pe controller ke action ko call karta hai

---

## 🔹 Templated HTML Helper ka summary

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: One-line me batao Templated helper kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

👉 **Templated HTML Helper** =
**Data type ke basis par automatically correct control generate karne wala helper**

---

## 🔹 Comparison (Final clarity)

| Helper Type        | Key Point                       |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------- |
| **Standard**       | Manual control selection        |
| **Strongly Typed** | Model bound but manual control  |
| ✅ **Templated**    | **Automatic control selection** |

---

## 🔹 Next Topic 🚀

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Next video me kya aane wala hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, next topic hai:

👉 **Custom HTML Helpers**

* Apna khud ka helper banana
* Reusable components

---

## ✅ Final Takeaway

> **EditorFor + DisplayFor + Model Data Type =
> Clean, smart & professional MVC forms**

---

Agar chaho to main:

* **Templated Signup Form**
* **Standard vs Strong vs Templated (Interview table)**
* **MVC Helpers quick revision notes**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

# 📘 Part 21: **Creating Custom HTML Helpers**

ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## 🔹 Recap (Pichhle helpers ka quick recap)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab tak hum kaun-kaun se HTML Helpers dekh chuke hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, humne ye helpers dekhe:

1. **Standard HTML Helpers**
2. **Strongly Typed HTML Helpers**
3. **Templated HTML Helpers**

Aur aaj ka topic hai:
👉 **Custom HTML Helpers**

---

## 🔹 Custom HTML Helper kyun chahiye?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Jab itne helpers already hain, fir Custom helper ki zarurat kyun?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, problem ye hai ki:

* **Button** ke liye helper nahi hota
* **Image (img tag)** ke liye helper nahi hota
* **Table, custom layout, custom UI** ke liye helper nahi hota

👉 Aise cases me hum **khud ka helper banate hain**, jise bolte hain
**Custom HTML Helper**

---

## 🔹 Custom HTML Helper kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Simple language me Custom HTML Helper kya hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir,
👉 **Custom HTML Helper** =
Ek aisa helper jo **hum khud banate hain**, taaki HTML tag **reuse + clean code** ke saath generate ho sake.

---

## 🔹 Custom Helper banane ke rules 📌

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Custom HTML Helper banane ke rules kya hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

1. **Static class** banani hoti hai
2. Us class ke andar **static methods** hote hain
3. Method ka **return type hamesha `HtmlString` hota hai**
4. Method ke andar **HTML tag string form me return hota hai**

---

## 🔹 Step 1: Static class banana

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Custom helper class kahan banate hain?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir:

* Na Model folder me
* Na Controller folder me
* Na View folder me

👉 **Direct project ke root me class banate hain**

### 📌 Example: `MyHelper.cs`

```csharp
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Html;

public static class MyHelper
{
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 2: Button ke liye Custom Helper

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Button ke liye helper kaise banayenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, button me usually 2 cheezein hoti hain:

* **type** (submit / reset / button)
* **value** (text)

### 📌 Button Helper Method

```csharp
public static HtmlString Button(string type, string value)
{
    return new HtmlString(
        string.Format("<input type='{0}' value='{1}' />", type, value)
    );
}
```

### 🔑 Important points:

* `{0}` → `type`
* `{1}` → `value`
* `string.Format()` se HTML tag generate hota hai

---

## 🔹 Step 3: View me Custom Button use karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Is helper ko View me kaise use karenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

### 📌 Razor View code

```csharp
@MyHelper.Button("submit", "Submit")
<br />
@MyHelper.Button("reset", "Reset")
```

### ✅ Output:

* Ek **Submit button**
* Ek **Reset button**

---

## 🔹 Generated HTML (View Source me)

```html
<input type="submit" value="Submit" />
<input type="reset" value="Reset" />
```

👉 Ye HTML **automatically helper se generate** hua.

---

## 🔹 Step 4: Image ke liye Custom Helper

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Image ke liye helper kaise banayenge?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir, image ke liye generally chahiye:

* **src** (mandatory)
* **alt** (optional)
* **width** (optional)
* **height** (optional)

---

### 📌 Image Helper Method

```csharp
public static HtmlString Image(
    string src,
    string alt = "",
    string width = "",
    string height = ""
)
{
    return new HtmlString(
        string.Format(
            "<img src='{0}' alt='{1}' width='{2}' height='{3}' />",
            src, alt, width, height
        )
    );
}
```

### 🔑 Yahan:

* `src` → compulsory
* `alt`, `width`, `height` → **optional parameters**

---

## 🔹 Step 5: Image helper use karna

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: View me Image helper kaise call hoga?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

```csharp
@MyHelper.Image("/images/logo.png", "Company Logo", "100", "100")
```

### Ya sirf src ke saath:

```csharp
@MyHelper.Image("/images/logo.png")
```

👉 Width/Height nahi doge to default image size aayega.

---

## 🔹 Generated HTML (Image)

```html
<img src="/images/logo.png" alt="Company Logo" width="100" height="100" />
```

---

## 🔹 Optional parameters ka fayda

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Optional parameters kyun use kiye?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**
Sir:

* User **sirf required cheez pass** kare
* Baaki cheez **default value se manage** ho jaye

👉 Code **flexible + reusable** ho jata hai

---

## 🔹 Custom HTML Helper ka real use case

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Real project me Custom Helper kyun important hai?

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

* Same **button style** bar-bar chahiye
* Same **image tag** bar-bar chahiye
* Common **HTML structure** reuse karna ho

👉 Tab **Custom HTML Helper best solution** hai

---

## 🔹 Summary – Custom HTML Helper 🧠

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Custom HTML Helper ka short summary do

**👨‍🎓 Student:**

✔ Jab built-in helper available na ho
✔ Jab repeated HTML likhna avoid karna ho
✔ Jab clean & reusable code chahiye

👉 **Static class + static method + HtmlString**

---

## 🔹 Complete Helper Types (Final list)

| Helper Type        | Purpose                       |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------- |
| **Standard**       | Basic controls                |
| **Strongly Typed** | Model-based controls          |
| **Templated**      | Data-type based auto controls |
| ✅ **Custom**       | Developer-defined controls    |

---

## 🎯 Final Takeaway

> **Custom HTML Helper =
> Apni marzi ka HTML generator, reusable aur clean code ke saath**

---

Agar chaho to main next me:

* **Interview Q&A on HTML Helpers**
* **Standard vs Strong vs Templated vs Custom (One table)**
* **Real project example with Custom Helper**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

# 📘 Part 22 – Scaffolding for CRUD in ASP.NET Core MVC

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Scaffolding** ASP.NET Core MVC ka ek **feature** hai jiske through hum **automatically code generate** kar sakte hain.
Matlab:

* Hume sab kuch **manually likhne ki zarurat nahi**
* Visual Studio ke **ready-made templates** use karke
* **CRUD (Create, Read, Update, Delete)** ka code auto-generate ho jata hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: CRUD ka full form kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**CRUD** ka full form hai:

* **Create** – new data insert karna
* **Read** – data dekhna / list karna
* **Update** – existing data change karna
* **Delete** – data remove karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pehle CRUD ke liye kya problem thi?

**Student:**
Sir, pehle:

* Har operation ke liye **alag-alag View banana**
* **Forms likhna**
* **HTML + Razor code** manually likhna
  bahut **time-consuming** tha 😓

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding se kya fayda hota hai?

**Student:**

### ✅ Benefits of Scaffolding

* ⏱️ **Time save hota hai**
* 📄 **Boilerplate code auto generate**
* 🧩 **Standard structure follow hota hai**
* 👶 Beginners ke liye **bahut easy**

---

# 🧱 Project Setup

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding start karne ke liye pehla step kya hai?

**Student:**
Pehla step hai:
👉 **ASP.NET Core MVC Project create karna**

Example:

* Project Name: `MvcDemo` (kuch bhi rakh sakte ho)

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Model Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding kis cheez ke base par hoti hai?

**Student:**
**Model class** ke base par 👍
Isliye sabse pehle **Model banana zaroori** hai.

---

### 🧪 Example: Student Model

```csharp
public class Student
{
    public int Roll { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public string Gender { get; set; }
    public DateTime DOB { get; set; }
    public string EmailId { get; set; }
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Labels (field names) ko user-friendly kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Iske liye hum **Data Annotations** use karte hain, jaise **Display**.

---

### 🧪 Display Attribute Example

```csharp
[Display(Name = "Roll Number")]
public int Roll { get; set; }

[Display(Name = "Student Name")]
public string Name { get; set; }

[Display(Name = "Date Of Birth")]
public DateTime DOB { get; set; }
```

👉 Ye sirf **View ke labels** change karta hai, database se koi lena-dena nahi.

---

# 🧩 Step 2: Controller Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Data View ko kaun handle karta hai?

**Student:**
**Controller** handle karta hai 👍

Hum:

* **StudentController** banate hain
* Uske andar **Index action method** likhte hain

---

### 🧪 Index Action Method (List ke liye)

```csharp
public IActionResult Index()
{
    List<Student> students = new List<Student>()
    {
        new Student{ Roll=1, Name="Amit", Gender="Male", DOB=DateTime.Now, EmailId="amit@test.com"},
        new Student{ Roll=2, Name="Suman", Gender="Female", DOB=DateTime.Now, EmailId="suman@test.com"}
    };

    return View(students);
}
```

👉 Kyunki multiple students dikhane hain, isliye:

* **List<Student>** use kiya

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Scaffolding se View Generate karna (List)

---

## ❓ Teacher: List View manually banani padegi?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir
Hum **Scaffolding** use karenge 😄

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding ke steps kya hain?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Steps:

1. **Action Method (Index)** par right-click
2. **Add View**
3. **Razor View** select karo
4. **Template = List**
5. **Model = Student**
6. **Add** button click

---

## ❓ Teacher: Isse kya hoga?

**Student:**

* Automatically **Index.cshtml** generate hoga
* Table, headings, loop — sab ready 🎉

---

## ❓ Teacher: List View me kya-kya aata hai?

**Student:**
Auto-generated List View me:

* **Table format**
* **Student ka data**
* **Action links**:

  * **Edit**
  * **Details**
  * **Delete**
* **Create New** button

---

# 🧩 Step 4: Create View (Insert Form)

---

## ❓ Teacher: “Create New” button kaise kaam karta hai?

**Student:**
“Create New”:

* **Create action method** ko call karta hai
* Isliye controller me **Create()** banana padta hai

---

### 🧪 Create Action Method

```csharp
public IActionResult Create()
{
    return View();
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Create View scaffolding se kaise banate hain?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Steps:

1. **Create() method** par right-click
2. **Add View**
3. **Razor View**
4. **Template = Create**
5. **Model = Student**
6. **Add**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Create View me kya milega?

**Student:**

* Auto-generated **Form**
* TextBox, Date picker, Email field
* **Create** & **Back to List** buttons

👉 Form submit abhi kaam nahi karega
(kyunki database / EF abhi use nahi kiya)

---

# 🧩 Step 5: Edit View (Update)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit ka kaam kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Edit**:

* Existing record ko **update** karta hai
* Isliye **ID / Roll Number** pass hota hai

---

### 🧪 Edit Action Method

```csharp
public IActionResult Edit(int id)
{
    return View();
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit View kaise generate hota hai?

**Student:**
Scaffolding steps same hain, bas:

* **Template = Edit**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form empty kyun aa raha tha?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* Form ko **Student object mila hi nahi**
* Data **search karke View ko pass** nahi kiya

---

## ❓ Teacher: Data Edit form me kaise aaya?

**Student:**

* Student list ko **class-level variable** banaya
* **ID ke base par search** kiya
* Matching Student object ko **View(student)** pass kiya

---

### 🧪 Search Logic Example

```csharp
Student std = students.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Roll == id);
return View(std);
```

👉 Ab Edit form **pre-filled** aayega ✅

---

# 🧩 Step 6: Details View

---

## ❓ Teacher: Details ka use kya hai?

**Student:**
**Details View**:

* Sirf **read-only information** dikhata hai
* Koi editing nahi

---

## ❓ Teacher: Details View ka template kaunsa hota hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Template = Details**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Data Details me kaise dikhta hai?

**Student:**

* Same logic:

  * ID receive
  * Student search
  * Student object View ko pass

---

# 🧩 Step 7: Delete View

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete View ka role kya hai?

**Student:**
Delete View:

* User se **confirmation** leta hai
  👉 *“Are you sure you want to delete?”*

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete ka template kaunsa hota hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Template = Delete**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete View me data kaise aata hai?

**Student:**

* ID receive hoti hai
* Student search hota hai
* Student object View ko pass hota hai
* Details show hoti hain + Delete button

---

# 🔑 Important Learning Points

---

## ❓ Teacher: Scaffolding ke total kaun-kaun se templates hote hain?

**Student:**

### 🧩 Scaffolding Templates:

1. **List**
2. **Create**
3. **Edit**
4. **Details**
5. **Delete**

👉 Ye 5 templates milkar **CRUD complete** kar dete hain

---

## ❓ Teacher: Abhi sirf List me data kyun dikh raha tha?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* List View ko **List<Student>** mila tha
* Baaki views ko **single Student object** chahiye hota hai
* Isliye search logic lagana padta hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Real application me ye sab kaise kaam karega?

**Student:**
Jab hum:

* **Entity Framework**
* **Database**
  use karenge, tab:
* Data permanent store hoga
* Edit / Delete / Create fully work karega

---

# ✅ Final Summary

---

### 🔹 Scaffolding ka matlab:

* **Auto code generation**
* CRUD ke liye **ready-made Views**
* Beginner ke liye **best feature**

### 🔹 Yaad rakhna:

* **Model → Controller → Scaffolding View**
* List = **Multiple objects**
* Edit / Details / Delete = **Single object (ID based)**

# 📘 Part 23 – Validating Data in Loosely Typed Form (ASP.NET Core MVC)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pichhle topics me hum kya-kya padh chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, humne:

* **HTML Helper Classes** dekhi (form easily banane ke liye)
* **Scaffolding** dekha (auto code generate karne ke liye)

Lekin ek **bahut important cheez** abhi baaki thi 👉 **Validation**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation kyun zaroori hoti hai?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki:

* User **kuch bhi type** kar sakta hai
* Form **khali chhod kar submit** kar sakta hai
* Email ka **wrong format** ho sakta hai
* Mobile number **kam / zyada digits** ka ho sakta hai

Agar validation nahi lagayi:

* ❌ Database me **galat ya bekaar data** chala jaata hai
* ❌ Data ka **koi use nahi rehta**

Isliye **validation mandatory** hai 👍

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation ka matlab simple language me kya hai?

**Student:**
Validation ka matlab:

> **Data ko store karne se pehle check karna**
> ki:

* Data **required hai ya nahi**
* Data **sahi format** me hai ya nahi
* Data **correct range / length** me hai ya nahi

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation ke types kaun-kaun se hote hain?

**Student:**
Validation mainly **2 types** ki hoti hai:

### 1️⃣ **Client Side Validation**

* Browser side par hoti hai
* JavaScript se hoti hai
* Fast hoti hai
* Lekin **secure nahi hoti** (JS disable ho sakti hai)

### 2️⃣ **Server Side Validation** ✅

* Server (Controller) par hoti hai
* **Secure** hoti hai
* Data Controller tak jaata hai, fir check hota hai

👉 Is video me hum **Server Side Validation** padh rahe hain

---

## ❓ Teacher: ASP.NET Core MVC me Server Side Validation kaise hoti hai?

**Student:**
Server side validation hoti hai:

* **Model Validation** ke through

Matlab:

* Validation ka code **Model class** me likhte hain
* **Attributes (Data Annotations)** use karte hain
* Controller me **ModelState.IsValid** check karte hain

---

# 🧩 Validation Attributes (Data Annotations)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation ke liye kaun-kaun se attributes use hote hain?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Common Validation Attributes:

* **[Required]** → Field khali nahi ho sakta
* **[StringLength]** → String ki length control
* **[EmailAddress]** → Email format check
* **[Range]** → Min–Max value check
* **[Compare]** → Do fields compare karna (Password / Confirm Password)
* **[RegularExpression]** → Custom format
* **[Url]** → URL format check

---

# 🧩 Project Setup (Loosely Typed Form)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Is demo me hum kaunsa type ka form bana rahe hain?

**Student:**
Sir, hum bana rahe hain:
👉 **Loosely Typed Form**

Matlab:

* HTML Helpers use honge
* **@model strongly bind nahi hoga**
* Form manually design hoga

---

## ❓ Teacher: Sabse pehla step kya hoga?

**Student:**
👉 **MVC Project create karna**

Example:

* Project Name: `FormValidationDemo`

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Model Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation ka base kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Model class** 👍
Isliye pehle **Student model** banate hain.

---

### 🧪 Student Model (Without Validation)

```csharp
public class Student
{
    public int Roll { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public DateOnly DOB { get; set; }
    public string Email { get; set; }
    public string Mobile { get; set; }
}
```

---

# 🧩 Step 2: Validation Attributes Lagana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Roll aur Name compulsory kaise banayenge?

**Student:**
**[Required]** attribute se.

---

### 🧪 Validation ke saath Model

```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

public class Student
{
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Roll number cannot be blank")]
    [Display(Name = "Roll Number")]
    public int Roll { get; set; }

    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Name is required")]
    [Display(Name = "Student Name")]
    public string Name { get; set; }

    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Date of Birth is required")]
    [Display(Name = "Date Of Birth")]
    public DateOnly DOB { get; set; }

    [Required]
    [EmailAddress(ErrorMessage = "Invalid Email format")]
    [Display(Name = "Email ID")]
    public string Email { get; set; }

    [StringLength(10, ErrorMessage = "Mobile number must be 10 digits")]
    [Display(Name = "Mobile Number")]
    public string Mobile { get; set; }
}
```

👉 **Display** sirf label change karta hai
👉 **Required / EmailAddress / StringLength** validation ke liye hain

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Controller se Model Pass Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form ko model kaise milega?

**Student:**
Controller ke **Index (GET)** method se:

```csharp
public IActionResult Index()
{
    return View(new Student());
}
```

---

# 🧩 Step 4: Loosely Typed Form Banana (View)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
HTML Helpers se, jaise:

* **Html.BeginForm**
* **Html.TextBox**
* **Html.Editor**
* **Html.Label**

---

### 🧪 Form Structure (Index.cshtml)

```cshtml
@using (Html.BeginForm("Index", "Home", FormMethod.Post))
{
    @Html.Label("Roll")
    @Html.TextBox("Roll", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
    @Html.ValidationMessage("Roll", "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

    @Html.Label("Name")
    @Html.TextBox("Name", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
    @Html.ValidationMessage("Name", "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

    @Html.Label("DOB")
    @Html.Editor("DOB", new { htmlAttributes = new { @class = "form-control" } })
    @Html.ValidationMessage("DOB", "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

    @Html.Label("Email")
    @Html.TextBox("Email", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
    @Html.ValidationMessage("Email", "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

    @Html.Label("Mobile")
    @Html.TextBox("Mobile", "", new { @class = "form-control" })
    @Html.ValidationMessage("Mobile", "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

    <input type="submit" value="Submit" class="btn btn-primary" />
}
```

👉 **ValidationMessage** wahi jagah hai jahan error show hota hai

---

# 🧩 Step 5: POST Method & ModelState Validation

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation actually kab check hoti hai?

**Student:**
Jab form **submit hota hai** aur **POST method** call hota hai.

---

### 🧪 POST Action Method

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Index(Student student)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        return RedirectToAction("Details", student);
    }

    return View(student);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: ModelState.IsValid kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* **ModelState** = Model ka current state (properties + values)
* **IsValid = true** → sab validation pass
* **IsValid = false** → koi validation fail

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation fail hone par kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* Same form **dobara open hota hai**
* **Error messages show** hote hain
* Jo data user ne dala tha, wo **pre-filled** rehta hai

---

# 🧩 Step 6: Successful Data Show Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar sab data valid ho jaye to kya karein?

**Student:**
Ek alag action me data show kara dete hain.

---

### 🧪 Details Action

```csharp
public IActionResult Details(Student student)
{
    return View(student);
}
```

👉 Iske liye hum **Details template (Scaffolding)** use kar sakte hain

---

# 🧠 Important Points to Remember

---

## ✅ Summary:

* Validation **Model level** par hoti hai
* **Data Annotations** se validation lagate hain
* Error show karne ke liye:

  * `@Html.ValidationMessage("PropertyName")`
* Controller me:

  * `ModelState.IsValid` **must check**
* Loosely Typed Form me:

  * Form manually banta hai
  * Validation fir bhi **model se hi hoti hai**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Next topic me kya aane wala hai?

**Student:**
Sir, next video me:
👉 **Same form ko Strongly Typed banayenge**
👉 Validation ka logic **same rahega**, sirf form change hoga 😄

---

Agar chaho to main:

* **Loosely vs Strongly Typed comparison**
* **Interview questions on Validation**
* **Real project validation examples**

bhi bana deta hoon 👍

# 📘 Part 24 – Validating Data in **Strongly Typed Form** (ASP.NET Core MVC)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pichhle video me humne kya dekha tha?

**Student:**
Sir, pichhle video me humne **Loosely Typed Form** me **Server Side Validation** dekhi thi —

* **Model me attributes**
* **Controller me `ModelState.IsValid`**
* **Validation messages View me**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Aaj hum dekhenge:
👉 **Strongly Typed View** ke saath **Validation** kaise hoti hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Loosely aur Strongly Typed form me main difference kya hota hai?

**Student:**

### 🔹 **Loosely Typed Form**

* Field names **string ke form me** likhte hain
* Example: `"Name"`, `"Email"`

### 🔹 **Strongly Typed Form** ✅

* Direct **Model properties** bind hoti hain
* **Lambda expression** use hota hai
* Example: `m => m.Name`, `m => m.Email`

👉 Validation **same hi rehti hai**, sirf **form likhne ka style change** hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Strongly Typed Form ka fayda kya hai?

**Student:**

* ✅ **Compile-time error checking**
* ✅ **Refactoring easy**
* ✅ **Less spelling mistakes**
* ✅ **Clean & readable code**

---

# 🧩 Important Rule (Yaad rakhna!)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kya Model validation me kuch change hota hai?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir.
**Model bilkul same rehta hai**.

👉 **Data Annotations (Attributes)**:

* `[Required]`
* `[EmailAddress]`
* `[StringLength]`
* `[Display]`

sab **waise ke waise hi** rahenge.

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Strongly Typed View Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: View ko Strongly Typed kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
View ke top par likhte hain:

```cshtml
@model ValidationDemo.Models.Student
```

👉 Isse View ko pata chal jaata hai ki:

* Ye **Student model** ke saath bind hai

---

# 🧩 Step 2: HTML Helpers (Strongly Typed)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Loosely typed helpers ko strongly typed kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Bas **`For` version** use karni hoti hai 👍

---

### 🔁 Mapping (Very Important)

| Loosely Typed         | Strongly Typed               |
| --------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| `Label()`             | **`LabelFor()`**             |
| `TextBox()`           | **`TextBoxFor()`**           |
| `Editor()`            | **`EditorFor()`**            |
| `ValidationMessage()` | **`ValidationMessageFor()`** |

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Strongly Typed Form Example

---

## ❓ Teacher: Strongly Typed Form ka example dikhao.

**Student:**

```cshtml
@model ValidationDemo.Models.Student

@using (Html.BeginForm())
{
    <div>
        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Roll)
        @Html.TextBoxFor(m => m.Roll, new { @class = "form-control" })
        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Roll, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
    </div>

    <div>
        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Name)
        @Html.TextBoxFor(m => m.Name, new { @class = "form-control" })
        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Name, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
    </div>

    <div>
        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.DOB)
        @Html.EditorFor(m => m.DOB, new { htmlAttributes = new { @class = "form-control" } })
        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.DOB, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
    </div>

    <div>
        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Email)
        @Html.TextBoxFor(m => m.Email, new { @class = "form-control" })
        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Email, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
    </div>

    <div>
        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Mobile)
        @Html.TextBoxFor(m => m.Mobile, new { @class = "form-control" })
        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Mobile, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
    </div>

    <input type="submit" value="Submit" class="btn btn-primary" />
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: `m => m.Name` ka matlab kya hai?

**Student:**

* `m` = **Model object**
* `m.Name` = **Model ki property**

👉 Ye **Lambda Expression** hai
👉 Isse **strong binding** hoti hai

---

# 🧩 Step 4: Controller (No Change!)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Controller me kuch change karna padega?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir.
Controller **bilkul same** rahega.

---

### 🧪 POST Action (Same as before)

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Index(Student student)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        return RedirectToAction("Details", student);
    }

    return View(student);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation kaha actually check hoti hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Controller** me
👉 `ModelState.IsValid`

* **True** → sab validation pass
* **False** → error ke saath form wapas

---

# 🧩 Step 5: Output Behavior

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar form blank submit karein to kya hoga?

**Student:**

* Validation fail hogi ❌
* **Error messages** show honge
* User ka dala hua data **pre-filled** rahega

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar sab data sahi ho?

**Student:**

* Redirect hoga **Details page** par
* Data properly show ho jayega ✅

---

# 🧩 Small Practical Tip

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kabhi-kabhi Date field properly render nahi hoti, tab kya karein?

**Student:**
Sir, kabhi-kabhi:

* `EditorFor` ya scaffolding me Date control sahi nahi aata

👉 Solution:

* **Manually `EditorFor` ya `TextBoxFor` add kar do**
* HTML/CSS adjust kar lo

---

# 🧩 Extra Topic Mentioned (Next Video)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Validation Summary:

* Saare **error messages ek hi jagah** show karta hai
* Alag-alag `ValidationMessageFor` likhne ki zarurat nahi

👉 Isko **next video** me detail me dekhenge 👍

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (Exam / Interview Friendly)

---

### ✅ Strongly Typed Validation Key Points:

* **Model validation same** rehti hai
* Sirf **View strongly typed** hota hai
* **For helpers** use hote hain:

  * `LabelFor`
  * `TextBoxFor`
  * `EditorFor`
  * `ValidationMessageFor`
* **Safe, clean, professional approach**

---

**Student:**
👉 **Validation Summary**
👉 Single place par saare error messages dikhana

# 📘 Part 25 – Validation Summary (ASP.NET Core MVC)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Abhi tak validation me hum kya-kya dekh chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, abhi tak humne dekha:

1. **Loosely Typed Form** me validation
2. **Strongly Typed Form** me validation
3. **Field-wise validation messages** using

   * `ValidationMessageFor`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka naya topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Aaj hum dekhenge **Validation Summary** 👉
Matlab **saare validation errors ek hi jagah** show karna, instead of har field ke niche alag-alag error.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary ka real-life use kya hai?

**Student:**
Sir, bahut saare forms me:

* 2–3 fields me error hota hai
* User ko scroll karke dekhna padta hai

👉 **Validation Summary** me:

* Saare errors **ek consolidated list** me dikh jaate hain
* Usually **form ke top ya bottom** me

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Project & Model Setup

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary ke liye pehla step kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sabse pehle **Model** banana padta hai, kyunki:

* Validation **model ke attributes** se hi hoti hai

---

### 🧱 Example: `Customer` Model

```csharp
public class Customer
{
    [Display(Name = "Customer ID")]
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Customer ID cannot be blank")]
    public int CustomerId { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Customer Name")]
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Customer Name cannot be blank")]
    public string CustomerName { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Date of Birth")]
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Please select Date of Birth")]
    public DateTime DOB { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Mobile Number")]
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Mobile Number is required")]
    public string Mobile { get; set; }

    [Display(Name = "Email ID")]
    [Required(ErrorMessage = "Email is required")]
    [EmailAddress(ErrorMessage = "Please enter valid Email format")]
    public string Email { get; set; }
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation ka logic kaha likha gaya hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Model ke andar**
👉 Using **Data Annotations**:

* **`[Required]`**
* **`[EmailAddress]`**
* **`[Display]`**

---

# 🧩 Step 2: Scaffolded Create View (Index)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form kaise banaya gaya?

**Student:**
Sir, humne:

* `HomeController → Index` par
* **Scaffolding (Create template)** use ki

Isse:

* Form auto-generate ho gaya
* Field-wise validation messages bhi aa gaye

---

## ❓ Teacher: Problem kya thi field-wise validation me?

**Student:**
Problem ye thi ki:

* Har field ke niche alag-alag error aa raha tha
* Agar 4 errors hain → 4 jagah dekhna padta

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Validation Summary Kya Hai?

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary exactly hota kya hai?

**Student:**
**Validation Summary**:

* Saare validation errors ko
* **Ek hi jagah list form me** show karta hai

👉 Example:

```
Customer ID cannot be blank
Customer Name cannot be blank
Email format is invalid
```

---

# 🧩 Step 4: Validation Summary using HTML Helper

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary helper ka naam kya hai?

**Student:**
👉 **`Html.ValidationSummary()`**

---

### ✍️ Syntax (Helper Class)

```cshtml
@Html.ValidationSummary()
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Isko kaha likhte hain?

**Student:**
Usually:

* **Submit button ke upar** ya
* **Form ke top** me

---

### ✅ Example with Bootstrap styling

```cshtml
<div class="text-danger border border-danger p-2 mb-3">
    @Html.ValidationSummary()
</div>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab field-wise validation messages ka kya karein?

**Student:**
Agar **sirf summary chahiye**, to:

* Individual `ValidationMessageFor` **remove kar sakte hain**

---

# 🧩 Step 5: Controller POST Action (Important)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary kaam tab karegi jab kya hoga?

**Student:**
Jab:

* Form **POST** hoga
* Controller me **`ModelState.IsValid`** check hoga

---

### 🧠 Controller Code

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Index(Customer customer)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        return RedirectToAction("ShowData", customer);
    }

    return View(customer);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar `[HttpPost]` na lagaye to kya hoga?

**Student:**
Sir, error aayega:

* **Multiple actions found for Index**
* MVC confuse ho jaata hai (GET vs POST)

👉 Isliye `[HttpPost]` **mandatory** hai

---

# 🧩 Step 6: ShowData View

---

## ❓ Teacher: Valid data submit hone par kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* Redirect hota hai **ShowData action** par
* Waha data display hota hai

---

### 🧩 ShowData Action

```csharp
public IActionResult ShowData(Customer customer)
{
    return View(customer);
}
```

---

### 🧩 ShowData View

* **Details template** se scaffold kiya
* Customer ka data display ho gaya

---

# 🧩 Step 7: Validation Summary using Tag Helper (Alternate Way)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kya Validation Summary sirf helper se hi hoti hai?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir
ASP.NET Core me **Tag Helper** bhi hota hai

---

### ✍️ Syntax (Tag Helper)

```html
<div asp-validation-summary="All" class="text-danger"></div>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: `asp-validation-summary="All"` ka matlab?

**Student:**

* **All** → saare errors dikhaye
* (Model-level + Field-level)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Helper vs Tag Helper me difference?

**Student:**

| Helper                      | Tag Helper                           |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `@Html.ValidationSummary()` | `<div asp-validation-summary="All">` |
| Razor syntax                | HTML-like syntax                     |
| Old style                   | Modern ASP.NET Core                  |

👉 **Dono ka output same hota hai**

---

# 🧩 Styling Validation Summary

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation Summary ko red box / border kaise dein?

**Student:**
Bootstrap ya CSS se:

```html
<div asp-validation-summary="All"
     class="text-danger border border-danger p-3 mb-3">
</div>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Jaise-jaise error fix karein, kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* Error list **automatically update hoti hai**
* Jo field fix ho jaati hai → uska error **gayab**

---

# 🧠 Final Comparison

---

## ❓ Teacher: Normal validation vs Validation Summary?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Normal Validation

* Har field ke niche error
* `ValidationMessageFor`

### 🔹 Validation Summary ✅

* Ek jagah saare errors
* Clean & user-friendly

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (One-shot Revision)

---

### ✅ Validation Summary Key Points

* **Purpose**: All errors at one place
* **Two ways**:

  1. `@Html.ValidationSummary()`
  2. `<div asp-validation-summary="All">`
* Works with:

  * **Model validation**
  * **ModelState.IsValid**
* Styling possible with **Bootstrap / CSS**

* URLs kaise controller & action se map hote hain

**Teacher → Student conversation**, **Hindi-wali English (Hinglish)**, **step-by-step**, beginner-friendly ✅

# 📘 Part 26 – Routing in ASP.NET Core MVC

(**Conventional Routing & Attribute Routing**)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Sir, aaj ka topic hai **Routing** —
Routing ka matlab hota hai **URL ko Controller aur Action Method se map karna**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Routing itna important kyu hai?

**Student:**
Routing ke **bahut saare benefits** hote hain:

1. **User-friendly URLs** banate hain
2. **SEO friendly URLs** milte hain
3. URL ko **short & clean** bana sakte hain
4. **Controller / Action ke naam hide** kar sakte hain
5. Real-world websites me **har jagah use hota hai**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Request jab web application me aati hai to kya hota hai?

**Student:**

1. Request aati hai browser se
2. Wo jaati hai **Request Pipeline (Middleware)** me
3. Wahan se decide hota hai:

   * Kaunsa **Controller**
   * Kaunsa **Action Method**
4. Ye decision **Routing** karta hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Default MVC routing kaise kaam karti hai?

**Student:**
By default URL aisa hota hai:

```
/ControllerName/ActionMethod
```

Example:

```
/Student/Index
/Student/Create
/Student/Edit/1
```

Isse user ko pata chal jaata hai:

* Controller ka naam
* Action method ka naam ❌ (ye hum hide karna chahte hain)

---

# 🧠 Routing ka Main Idea

---

## ❓ Teacher: Routing ka main goal kya hota hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Custom URL pattern banana**, jaise:

```
/students
/add-student
/edit-student/1
```

instead of:

```
/Student/Index
/Student/Create
/Student/Edit/1
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Routing kitne types ki hoti hai?

**Student:**
Routing **2 types** ki hoti hai:

1. **Conventional Routing**
2. **Attribute Routing** ✅ (Most used)

---

# 🔹 PART 1: Conventional Routing

---

## ❓ Teacher: Conventional routing kaha likhi jaati hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Program.cs** file ke andar
👉 Request pipeline (middleware) me

---

## ❓ Teacher: Default routing code kaha hota hai?

**Student:**
`Program.cs` me by default ye hota hai:

```csharp
app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");
```

* Default controller = **Home**
* Default action = **Index**
* `id?` = **optional parameter**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Default Home Page kaise change karte hain?

**Student:**
Agar hum chahte hain ki website start hote hi:

```
StudentController → Index
```

open ho, to pattern change kar denge:

```csharp
pattern: "{controller=Student}/{action=Index}/{id?}"
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar Create page ko home banana ho to?

**Student:**

```csharp
pattern: "{controller=Student}/{action=Create}/{id?}"
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Custom URLs kaise banate hain (Conventional)?

**Student:**
Uske liye hume:

* Default routing hataani padti hai
* **Endpoints manually define** karne padte hain

---

### 🧩 Step 1: `UseEndpoints`

```csharp
app.UseEndpoints(endpoints =>
{
    endpoints.MapControllerRoute(
        name: "students",
        pattern: "students",
        defaults: new { controller = "Student", action = "Index" }
    );

    endpoints.MapControllerRoute(
        name: "create-student",
        pattern: "create-student",
        defaults: new { controller = "Student", action = "Create" }
    );
});
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Iska matlab kya hua?

**Student:**

* URL `/students` → `StudentController.Index`
* URL `/create-student` → `StudentController.Create`

👉 Ab URL me **Controller / Action ka naam nahi dikhega**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Parameter kaise pass karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
pattern: "edit-student/{id?}"
```

* `{id?}` = optional parameter
* URL example:

  ```
  /edit-student/5
  ```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Conventional routing ka drawback kya hai?

**Student:**
❌ **Bahut zyada code**
❌ Hard to maintain
❌ Har action ke liye mapping likhni padti hai

👉 Isliye real projects me rarely use hoti hai

---

# 🔹 PART 2: Attribute Routing (Most Important)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Attribute routing kya hoti hai?

**Student:**
👉 Routing **directly Controller ya Action ke upar attribute** ke form me likhte hain

---

## ❓ Teacher: Attribute routing kaha likhte hain?

**Student:**
👉 **Controller ke Action Method ke upar**

---

### 🧩 Example: StudentController

```csharp
public class StudentController : Controller
{
    [Route("students")]
    public IActionResult Index()
    {
        return View();
    }
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab kaunsa URL kaam karega?

**Student:**

```
/students
```

👉 Direct `Index()` method call hoga
👉 **No controller/action name in URL**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Create, Edit, Delete kaise map karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
[Route("create-student")]
public IActionResult Create() { }

[Route("edit-student/{id?}")]
public IActionResult Edit(int id) { }

[Route("delete-student/{id?}")]
public IActionResult Delete(int id) { }
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: `{id?}` ka matlab kya hai?

**Student:**

* `id` = parameter
* `?` = optional

Example:

```
/edit-student/3
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Attribute routing ke fayde kya hain?

**Student:**

✅ **Very less code**
✅ Easy to read
✅ Easy to maintain
✅ SEO-friendly
✅ URLs fully customizable
✅ Real-world projects me standard

---

## ❓ Teacher: Conventional vs Attribute Routing?

**Student:**

| Feature       | Conventional | Attribute         |
| ------------- | ------------ | ----------------- |
| Code          | Zyada        | Kam ✅             |
| Maintain      | Hard         | Easy              |
| Location      | Program.cs   | Controller/Action |
| Usage         | Old          | Modern ✅          |
| Real projects | Rare         | Mostly            |

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj industry me kaunsi routing use hoti hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Attribute Routing**
Conventional routing:

* Mostly **interview knowledge** ke liye
* Ya legacy projects me

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (Exam / Interview Ready)

---

### ✅ Routing kya hai?

* URL ko **Controller + Action** se map karna

### ✅ Routing ke types:

1. **Conventional Routing**
2. **Attribute Routing** ⭐

### ✅ Attribute Routing kyu best hai?

* Clean URLs
* Less code
* SEO friendly
* Secure (controller/action hide)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Next topic kya hoga?

**Student:**
👉 **Blank ASP.NET Core Project**

* MVC template ke bina
* Sab kuch **manually configure** karna

---

Agar chaho to next me main:

* **Routing interview questions**
* **Attribute routing best practices**
* **REST-style URL examples**
* **Real-world routing structure**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

=================================================================================================================
Bahut achha question 👍
Ye doubt **har beginner ko hota hai** jab wo **Attribute Routing** start karta hai.

Main **simple Hindi-wali English**, **step-by-step** me clear karta hoon 👇

---

# ❓ Question (tumhara doubt)

> **Agar hum Attribute Routing use karte hain,
> to Program.cs me kya likhna padta hai?**

---

# ✅ Short & Direct Answer (Yaad rakhne layak)

> 🔑 **Attribute Routing use karte ho to
> Program.cs me sirf `MapControllers()` likhna hota hai**

---

# 🧠 Ab detail me samjho (important)

## 🔹 Routing ka kaam kaun karta hai?

Routing ka kaam **2 jagah se hota hai**:

1. **Program.cs** → routing ko ON karta hai
2. **Controller / Action** → actual URL define karta hai

👉 Attribute routing me:

* **URL definition → Controller me hoti hai**
* **Program.cs → sirf enable karta hai**

---

# ✅ MINIMUM REQUIRED Program.cs (Attribute Routing)

```csharp
var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// MVC services
builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();

var app = builder.Build();

// Middleware
app.UseHttpsRedirection();
app.UseStaticFiles();

app.UseRouting();      // 🔴 Mandatory
app.UseAuthorization();

// 🔑 Attribute Routing ke liye yahi line chahiye
app.MapControllers();

app.Run();
```

✔ Bas itna hi enough hai
✔ **MapControllerRoute() ki zarurat nahi**

---

# ❌ Ye line Attribute Routing me nahi chahiye

```csharp
app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");
```

👉 Ye **Conventional Routing** ke liye hoti hai
👉 Attribute routing me **optional** / mostly remove kar dete hain

---

# 🧩 Attribute Routing Controller Example

```csharp
public class StudentController : Controller
{
    [Route("students")]
    public IActionResult Index()
    {
        return View();
    }

    [Route("create-student")]
    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View();
    }

    [Route("edit-student/{id}")]
    public IActionResult Edit(int id)
    {
        return View();
    }
}
```

👉 Ab kaam karega:

```
/students
/create-student
/edit-student/5
```

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake (Interview + Practical)

❌ Agar tum ye likh doge:

```csharp
app.MapControllers();
```

lekin controller me **[Route] attribute nahi hai**,
to URL **kaam nahi karega** ❌

---

# 🧠 Best Practice (Industry Standard)

### ✔ Option 1: Pure Attribute Routing (Recommended)

```csharp
app.UseRouting();
app.MapControllers();
```

Controllers me **saare routes attribute se define**

---

### ✔ Option 2: Mixed Routing (Rare case)

```csharp
app.UseRouting();

app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");

app.MapControllers();
```

👉 Jab:

* Kuch controllers attribute use karein
* Kuch default routing use karein

---

# 🧠 Interview One-Liner (Must remember)

> ❓ *Attribute Routing use karte time Program.cs me kya likhte hain?*
> ✅ **UseRouting() + MapControllers()**

---

# 🔁 Comparison (Quick Table)

| Routing Type | Program.cs             |
| ------------ | ---------------------- |
| Conventional | `MapControllerRoute()` |
| Attribute    | `MapControllers()`     |
| Both         | Dono                   |

---

# 🧠 Final Rule (Pin it in mind)

> 🔑 **URL define karna → Controller ka kaam**
> 🔑 **Routing enable karna → Program.cs ka kaam**

---

* **REST-style Attribute Routing**
* **[HttpGet], [HttpPost] routing**
* **Route prefix with `[Route("students")]`**
* **Real project routing structure**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

# 📘 Part 27 – ASP.NET Core MVC App using **Empty Project**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pichhle topic me hum kya complete kar chuke the?

**Student:**
Sir, pichhle topic me humne **Routing** complete kiya tha:

* Request pipeline kya hoti hai
* Home page kaise decide hota hai
* **Conventional routing** aur **Attribute routing** ka use

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka main topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Aaj hum seekhenge:
👉 **ASP.NET Core Empty Project Template** use karke
👉 **MVC application scratch se kaise banate hain**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab tak hum kaunsa template use kar rahe the?

**Student:**
Ab tak hum log mostly **ASP.NET Core MVC Template** use kar rahe the.

---

## ❓ Teacher: MVC Template ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
MVC Template ka matlab:

* Bahut saari cheezein **already bana-banaya** milti hain

  * Controllers
  * Views
  * Layout page
  * Routing setup
  * Navbar, footer, etc.

👉 Jaise **ready-made form** mil jaata hai, bas data bharna hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Real companies template kyu avoid karti hain?

**Student:**
Sir, badi companies:

* **Har cheez custom** banana chahti hain
* Extra code / unwanted files nahi chahiye
* Full control chahiye project structure par

Isliye wo **Empty Project** se start karti hain.

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Empty Project Create karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Empty Project kaunsa hota hai?

**Student:**
👉 **ASP.NET Core Empty** template

---

### 🪜 Steps:

1. **Create New Project**
2. Search karo: **ASP.NET Core Empty**
3. Select → **Next**
4. Project name (e.g. `EmptyDemo`)
5. **Create**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Empty Project me kya-kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sir, by default:

* ❌ Controllers folder nahi
* ❌ Models folder nahi
* ❌ Views folder nahi
* Sirf **Program.cs**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Run karne par kya output aata hai?

**Student:**
Run karne par browser me sirf ye dikhta hai:

```
Hello World!
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ye Hello World kahan se aa raha hai?

**Student:**
`Program.cs` ke is code se 👇

```csharp
app.MapGet("/", () => "Hello World!");
```

👉 Matlab:

* Agar **blank URL** (`/`) hit ho
* To ye **string response** return kar do

---

# 🧩 Step 2: MVC Structure Manually Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: MVC app banane ke liye pehla kaam kya hai?

**Student:**
👉 **MVC folders manually create karna**

---

### 📂 Required Folders:

1. **Models**
2. **Views**
3. **Controllers**

---

### 🪜 Steps:

* Project par right click
* **Add → New Folder**
* Naam rakho:

  * `Models`
  * `Views`
  * `Controllers`

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Controller Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab kya banayenge?

**Student:**
👉 **Controller**

---

### 🪜 Steps:

* Controllers folder par right click
* **Add → Controller**
* Select: **MVC Controller – Empty**
* Naam: `HomeController`

---

### 🧾 Code (by default):

```csharp
public class HomeController : Controller
{
    public IActionResult Index()
    {
        return View();
    }
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab View kaise banayenge?

**Student:**
👉 `Index()` method par right click
👉 **Add View**

---

### 🪜 View Creation:

* View name: `Index`
* Razor View
* Add

---

### ✏️ Index.cshtml me likh do:

```html
<h1>Welcome to the World of Empty MVC</h1>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab run karne par kya aayega?

**Student:**
❌ Abhi bhi **Hello World** hi aayega 😅

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kyun? Humne controller aur view to bana liya!

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki:

* Abhi tak humne **MVC services enable nahi ki**
* Application ko pata hi nahi:

  * Controller kahan hai
  * View kahan hai

---

# 🧩 Step 4: MVC Services Enable Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: MVC services kaun enable karta hai?

**Student:**
👉 Ye kaam hota hai **Services** ka

---

### 🧾 `Program.cs` me add karo:

```csharp
builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();
```

👉 Ye service:

* Controller ko View se link karti hai
* View search ka kaam karti hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Sirf service add karna kaafi hai?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir
Service ko **use bhi karna** padta hai

---

# 🧩 Step 5: Routing Enable Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Routing kaise enable karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
app.UseRouting();
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Default MVC routing kaise define karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");
```

---

### 🧠 Matlab:

* Default controller → **Home**
* Default action → **Index**
* `id?` → optional parameter

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab complete `Program.cs` ka flow kya hua?

**Student:**

1. MVC services add ki
2. Routing enable ki
3. Default route map ki

Ab application ko pata hai:

* Controller kahan milega
* View kahan milega

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab run karne par kya output aayega?

**Student:**
🎉 **Welcome to the World of Empty MVC**
(Index view render ho jayega)

---

# 🧩 Step 6: Navbar / Footer Kyun Nahi Dikh Raha?

---

## ❓ Teacher: MVC template me navbar, footer aata tha, yahan kyun nahi?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki:

* Ye **Empty Project** hai
* Abhi tak **Layout page** bana hi nahi

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page kya hota hai?

**Student:**

👉 **Layout Page** = Common structure

* Navbar
* Footer
* Common CSS / JS

Jo **har page me same** hota hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout ka use kyu karte hain?

**Student:**

* Code repetition kam hota hai
* Consistent UI milta hai
* Development fast hota hai
* Errors kam hote hain

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page ka topic kab aayega?

**Student:**
👉 **Next video / next topic** me
Detail me **_Layout.cshtml** banayenge

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (Interview Ready)

---

### ✅ Empty Project ka matlab:

* No MVC structure by default
* Sab kuch **manually banana padta hai**

### ✅ Steps to create MVC app in Empty Project:

1. Empty project create karo
2. MVC folders add karo
3. Controller banao
4. View banao
5. `AddControllersWithViews()`
6. `UseRouting()`
7. `MapControllerRoute()`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Next topic kya hoga?

**Student:**
👉 **Layout Page in ASP.NET Core MVC**

* Navbar
* Footer
* Shared views
* `_Layout.cshtml`

---

Agar chaho to main next me:

* **Layout page full practical**
* **Shared folder usage**
* **Real-world layout structure**
* **Interview Q&A on Layout**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊
