# 📘 Part 28 – **Understanding Layout in ASP.NET Core MVC**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ke video ka main topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Sir, aaj hum **Layout** ka **concept** samjhenge —

* Layout kya hota hai
* Kyun use hota hai
* Kaise kaam karta hai

👉 Is video me **implementation nahi**, sirf **concept clarity** hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab tak hum jab MVC template use kar rahe the, ek cheez notice ki?

**Student:**
Haan sir.
Hum **Index view** me sirf apna form ya content likhte the,
lekin web page par **automatically** ye cheezein dikh jaati thi:

* **Navigation bar (Header)**
* **Footer**
* Project ka name
* Privacy / Home links

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ye extra content humne likha hi nahi, phir aa kahan se raha hai?

**Student:**
Sir, ye sab aa raha hai **Layout Page** se.

---

# 🧠 Layout ka Concept

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout kya hota hai simple words me?

**Student:**
👉 **Layout ek master page hota hai**
Jisme wo saara **common content** hota hai jo:

* Har page par same rehta hai
* Jaise:

  * Header / Navbar
  * Footer
  * Banner
  * Sticky menu
  * Common CSS / JS

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pehle (old technologies) is concept ko kya bolte the?

**Student:**
Sir, pehle isko **Master Page** bolte the
(ASP.NET, JSP, PHP etc.).

MVC / ASP.NET Core me isi ko **Layout** bolte hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Website me Layout ki zarurat kyu hoti hai?

**Student:**

Agar website me **10 pages** hain:

* Har page me:

  * Navbar same
  * Footer same

Agar Layout na ho to:

* Har page me **same code repeat**
* **Copy-paste**
* Bug ke chances
* Time waste
* Performance slow

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout use karne ke fayde kya hain?

**Student:**

### ✅ Major Benefits of Layout:

1. **Code repetition kam**
2. **Maintenance easy**
3. **Time save**
4. **Bug chances kam**
5. **Performance better**
6. **Consistent UI**

---

# 📂 Layout File kahan hoti hai?

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout file MVC project me kahan milti hai?

**Student:**
👉 `Views → Shared → _Layout.cshtml`

Is file me hota hai:

* Navbar ka HTML
* Footer ka HTML
* Bootstrap / CSS
* Common structure

---

## ❓ Teacher: Navbar ka code kahan likha hota hai?

**Student:**
Sir, **_Layout.cshtml** ke andar.

Agar navbar ka text change karna ho (e.g. Project Name),
to **_Layout.cshtml** edit karna hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Footer ka text kahan se aata hai?

**Student:**
Footer ka code bhi **_Layout.cshtml** se hi aata hai.

---

# 🔗 Layout aur View ka Connection

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout har view par automatically kaise apply ho jata hai?

**Student:**
Sir, iska reason hai **_ViewStart.cshtml**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: _ViewStart.cshtml kya karta hai?

**Student:**
👉 Ye file batati hai ki:

> “Jab bhi koi view load ho, us par kaunsa layout apply karna hai”

---

### 🧾 `_ViewStart.cshtml` ka code:

```csharp
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
}
```

👉 Iska matlab:

* Har view ke liye **_Layout.cshtml** use hoga

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar hum _ViewStart.cshtml se Layout hata dein to?

**Student:**
To:

* Navbar gayab ❌
* Footer gayab ❌
* Sirf view ka content dikhega

---

# 🧩 Layout ko Selectively Apply Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar mujhe sab pages par layout nahi chahiye, to?

**Student:**
Sir, do options hote hain:

---

### 🔹 Option 1: **All pages ke liye Layout**

* `_ViewStart.cshtml` me likho
* Automatically sab views par apply

---

### 🔹 Option 2: **Sirf selected pages ke liye**

* `_ViewStart.cshtml` se layout remove kar do
* Jis view par chahiye, us view ke top par likh do:

```csharp
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Example?

**Student:**

* Index page me chahiye → likh do
* SendData page me nahi chahiye → mat likho

👉 Full control milta hai 👍

---

# 🧱 RenderBody – Sabse Important Part

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout file me view ka content exactly kahan jata hai?

**Student:**
Sir, **_Layout.cshtml** me ek special line hoti hai:

```csharp
@RenderBody()
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: RenderBody kya karta hai?

**Student:**
👉 **RenderBody** ek placeholder hai
Jahan:

* Index view ka content
* Ya kisi aur view ka content

**merge hota hai layout ke saath**

---

### 🧠 Simple samjho:

* Layout = Frame
* View = Picture
* RenderBody = Frame ke beech ka space

---

# 🎨 Layout ko Edit / Customize Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar navbar ka color change karna ho to?

**Student:**
Sir, **_Layout.cshtml** me jaakar:

* Bootstrap classes change kar do

  * `bg-light` → `bg-dark`
  * `text-dark` → `text-light`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar mujhe apna layout banana ho?

**Student:**
Tab:

* Existing layout ko edit kar sakte ho
* Ya **apna custom layout** bana sakte ho

👉 Next video me hum **khud ka layout banana** seekhenge.

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (Concept Clear)

---

### ✅ Layout kya hai?

* Ek **master page**
* Jisme **common content** hota hai

### ✅ Layout kahan hota hai?

* `Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml`

### ✅ Layout kaam kaise karta hai?

* `_ViewStart.cshtml` layout apply karta hai
* `@RenderBody()` view content ko inject karta hai

### ✅ Layout ke fayde:

* Less code
* Better performance
* Easy maintenance
* Consistent design

---

## ❓ Teacher: Next video me kya aane wala hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Layout Implementation**

* Khud ka layout banana
* Navbar, footer manually create karna
* Blank project me layout apply karna

---

Agar chaho to next step me main:

* **Custom layout full practical**
* **Multiple layouts**
* **Layout + sections**
* **Interview questions on Layout**

bhi bana deta hoon 😊

# 📘 Part 29 – **Creating & Implementing Custom Layout Page in ASP.NET Core MVC**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Is video ka main topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Sir, is video me hum **Custom Layout Page banana aur implement karna** seekhte hain.
Pichhle video me humne **Layout ka concept** samjha tha,
aur aaj **practically khud ka layout** bana rahe hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout ya Master Page ka concept dobara short me batao.

**Student:**
👉 Website = **multiple pages ka collection**
👉 Har page me:

* **Common content** (Navbar, Footer, Banner)
* **Page-specific content** (Form, Text, Data)

Pehle:

* Har page me same code repeat ❌

Ab:

* **Layout (Master Page)** me common content
* **View** me sirf page-specific content ✅

---

## 🏗️ Step 1: Empty (MT) Project Create Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj hum kaunsa project template use kar rahe hain?

**Student:**
Sir, hum **ASP.NET Core Empty (MT) Project** use kar rahe hain,
taaki **sab kuch scratch se** bana sakein.

---

### 🔹 Steps:

1. **Create New Project**
2. Select **ASP.NET Core Empty**
3. Project Name: `MyLayoutDemo`
4. Create

---

## ❓ Teacher: Empty project me by default kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* ❌ Models folder nahi
* ❌ Views folder nahi
* ❌ Controllers folder nahi

Sab kuch **manually banana padta hai**.

---

## 📂 Step 2: MVC Structure Manually Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: MVC ke liye kaun-kaun se folders chahiye?

**Student:**
Teen folders compulsory hote hain:

1. **Models**
2. **Views**
3. **Controllers**

---

### 🔹 Steps:

* Project par Right Click → Add → New Folder
* Create:

  * `Models`
  * `Controllers`
  * `Views`

---

## 🧭 Step 3: Controller Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Sabse pehle kya banana zaroori hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Controller**, kyunki request pehle controller ke paas jaati hai.

---

### 🔹 Steps:

* Controllers → Right Click → Add → Controller
* Select **MVC Controller – Empty**
* Name: **HomeController**

---

### 🔹 By default kya milta hai?

* `Index()` action method
* `Controller` base class inherit hoti hai
  (important namespace: `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc`)

---

## 🖼️ Step 4: View Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: HomeController ke Index action ke liye view kahan banega?

**Student:**
👉 Path hoga:

```
Views
 └── Home
     └── Index.cshtml
```

---

### 🔹 Steps:

* Views → Right Click → Add Folder → `Home`
* Home → Right Click → Add → View
* View Name: `Index`
* Add simple content:

```html
<h1>This is my Index Page</h1>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Abhi run karne par page kyu nahi khul raha?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki **routing & services enable nahi ki**.

---

## ⚙️ Step 5: Required Services & Routing Configure Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Controller aur View ko link karne ke liye kya chahiye?

**Student:**
👉 **Service enable karni padti hai**:

```csharp
builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Routing enable kaise karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
app.UseRouting();
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Default route kaise define karte hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
app.MapControllerRoute(
    name: "default",
    pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}"
);
```

---

## ✅ Result:

* App run karo
* **Home → Index view load ho jayega**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab header/footer kyu nahi dikh raha?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki ye **Empty project** hai,
yahan **koi default Layout nahi hota**.

---

# 🎨 Step 6: Custom Layout Page Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page kahan banana chahiye?

**Student:**
👉 Layout page hamesha hota hai:

```
Views
 └── Shared
     └── _Layout.cshtml
```

---

### 🔹 Steps:

1. Views → Right Click → Add → New Folder → `Shared`
2. Shared → Right Click → Add → New Item
3. Select **Razor Layout**
4. Name: `_Layout.cshtml`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page me sabse important cheez kya hoti hai?

**Student:**
👉 **@RenderBody()**

```csharp
@RenderBody()
```

Ye **placeholder** hota hai
jahan **view ka content inject hota hai**.

---

## 🧱 Step 7: Layout ke andar Common UI Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout me kya-kya banaya gaya?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Header / Navbar

* Company Name: **Indian School of Coding**
* Menu items:

  * Home
  * About Us
  * Courses
  * Gallery
  * Contact Us

### 🔹 Footer

* Copyright text

---

## ❓ Teacher: CSS kahan likhi?

**Student:**
Sir, **directly layout page ke andar `<style>` tag me**,
simple rakhne ke liye.

---

### 🔹 Example CSS concepts used:

* **Flexbox**
* `display: flex`
* `justify-content: space-between`
* `align-items: center`
* `background-color`
* `position: absolute` (footer bottom ke liye)

---

## 🔗 Step 8: Layout ko View par Apply Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout automatically apply kyu nahi ho raha?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki Empty project me **_ViewStart.cshtml nahi hota**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: To layout manually kaise apply karte hain?

**Student:**
Har view ke top par likhna hota hai:

```csharp
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
}
```

---

## ✅ Result:

* Header + Footer show hone lagta hai
* Beech me view ka content aata hai

---

## 📄 Step 9: Multiple Pages Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: About Us & Contact Us pages kaise banaye?

**Student:**

### Controller:

```csharp
public IActionResult AboutUs()
{
    return View();
}

public IActionResult ContactUs()
{
    return View();
}
```

---

### Views:

* `Views/Home/AboutUs.cshtml`
* `Views/Home/ContactUs.cshtml`

Har view me:

```csharp
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
}
<h1>About Us</h1>
```

---

## 🔗 Step 10: Navbar ko Clickable Links Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Navbar items ko page se kaise link kiya?

**Student:**
HTML `<a>` tags ka use kiya:

```html
<a href="/Home/Index">Home</a>
<a href="/Home/AboutUs">About Us</a>
<a href="/Home/ContactUs">Contact</a>
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Blue underline links kaise remove ki?

**Student:**
CSS use karke:

```css
a {
    text-decoration: none;
    color: white;
}
```

---

## 🧠 Final Understanding (Exam + Interview Ready)

---

### ✅ Layout kya hai?

* **Master Page**
* Common UI (Header, Footer, Banner)

### ✅ Layout kahan hota hai?

* `Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml`

### ✅ View content kahan inject hota hai?

* `@RenderBody()`

### ✅ Layout apply kaise hota hai?

* `_ViewStart.cshtml` (automatic)
* Ya view ke andar `Layout = "_Layout"` (manual)

### ✅ Empty project me kya seekha?

* MVC folders manually banana
* Routing & services configure karna
* Custom layout scratch se banana

---

👉 **Advanced Layout concepts**

* `_ViewStart.cshtml`
* Sections (`@RenderSection`)
* Multiple layouts
* Real-world layout structure

# 🏗️ Step 1: Empty (MT) Project Create Karna

## ❓ Teacher: Kaunsa project template use kiya gaya hai?

👉 **ASP.NET Core Empty (MT) Project**

Kyun?

* Taaki **layout, routing, images sab kuch scratch se** samajh aaye.

## 📂 Step 2: Required Folders Banana

## ❓ Teacher: MVC ke liye kaun-kaun se folders banane pade?

Aur Views ke andar:

* **Shared** (layout ke liye)

# 🎨 Step 3: _Layout.cshtml Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page kahan banta hai?

**Student:**
👉 `Views / Shared / _Layout.cshtml`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Layout page ka sabse important part kya hota hai?

```cshtml
@RenderBody()
```

Ye **placeholder** hota hai
jahan **har view ka content inject** hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Code readable banane ke liye kya use kiya?

```html
<!-- Navbar Start -->
<!-- Navbar End -->

<!-- Carousel Start -->
<!-- Carousel End -->
```

# 🎯 Step 4: Bootstrap CDN Add Karna

## ❓ Teacher: Bootstrap components use karne ke liye kya zaroori hai?

* **CSS CDN**
* **JS CDN**

Bootstrap website se copy-paste kiya.

---

# 🧭 Step 5: Bootstrap Navbar Add Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Navbar ka code kahan se liya?

**Student:**
👉 Bootstrap Docs → **Navbar component**
Direct **copy-paste** into `_Layout.cshtml`.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Navbar me kya-kya customize kiya?

**Student:**

* **Brand Name**:
  `Navbar` → **Indian School of Coding**

* **Menu Items**:

  * Home
  * About Us
  * Courses (Dropdown)

    * Basic Courses
    * Full Stack Courses
    * Skill Development
    * University Courses
  * Gallery
  * Contact Us

* **Button color**:

  * `btn-success` → `btn-primary`

---

# 🎞️ Step 6: Bootstrap Carousel Add Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Carousel kahan add kiya gaya?

**Student:**
👉 **Navbar ke niche**,
👉 **@RenderBody() se pehle**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Carousel ka code kahan se aaya?

**Student:**
Bootstrap Docs → **Carousel component**

* Auto-sliding carousel use kiya
* Indicators + controls included

---

## ❓ Teacher: Carousel auto slide kaise karta hai?

**Student:**
👉 `data-bs-interval`

Example:

```html
data-bs-interval="10000"  <!-- 10 seconds -->
data-bs-interval="2000"   <!-- 2 seconds -->
```

---

# 🖼️ Step 7: Images Use Karna (Most Important Part)

---

## ❓ Teacher: MT / Empty project me images use karna tricky kyun hota hai?

**Student:**
Kyunki:

* By default **static files enable nahi hoti**
* Path galat hone par image show nahi hoti

---

## ❓ Teacher: Images rakhne ke liye kaunsa folder banana padta hai?

**Student:**
👉 **wwwroot** (special folder)

Structure:

```
wwwroot
 └── images
     ├── pic1.jpg
     ├── pic2.jpg
     └── pic3.jpg
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: wwwroot folder special kyun hai?

**Student:**
👉 Jo bhi:

* **Images**
* **CSS**
* **JavaScript**

publicly accessible chahiye
wo **wwwroot** ke andar hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Image load hone ke liye Program.cs me kya enable karna hota hai?

**Student:**

```csharp
app.UseStaticFiles();
```

⚠️ Ye nahi likha → images show nahi hongi.

---

# 🔗 Step 8: Base Tag Use Karna (Important Trick)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Multiple pages par image path problem ka solution kya hai?

**Student:**
👉 **`<base>` tag**

Layout ke `<head>` me:

```html
<base href="/images/" />
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Base tag ka fayda kya hua?

**Student:**
Ab:

```html
<img src="pic1.jpg" />
```

Automatically resolve hoga:

```
/images/pic1.jpg
```

Chahe koi bhi page ho 👍

---

# 🧠 Step 9: Controller & Views Banana

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kaunsa controller banaya gaya?

**Student:**
👉 **HomeController**

Actions:

* `Index()`
* `AboutUs()`
* `Gallery()`
* `ContactUs()`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Views ka naming rule kya follow hua?

**Student:**

Controller: `HomeController`

Views path:

```
Views/Home/Index.cshtml
Views/Home/AboutUs.cshtml
Views/Home/Gallery.cshtml
Views/Home/ContactUs.cshtml
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Har view me kya likhna pada?

**Student:**
Sirf **page-specific content**, jaise:

```html
<h1>This is About Us Page</h1>
```

❌ Header / Footer likhne ki zarurat nahi.

---

# 🔁 Step 10: Layout Sab Views Par Auto Apply Karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Har view me `Layout = "_Layout"` likhna problem kyun hai?

**Student:**
Agar:

* 50–100 views ho gaye
  to har view me likhna **time waste** hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Is problem ka best solution kya hai?

**Student:**
👉 **_ViewStart.cshtml**

---

## ❓ Teacher: _ViewStart.cshtml kahan banta hai?

**Student:**
👉 `Views/_ViewStart.cshtml`

---

## ❓ Teacher: Isme kya code hota hai?

**Student:**

```cshtml
@{
    Layout = "_Layout";
}
```

---

## ✅ Result:

* Sab views par **automatically layout apply**
* Kisi view me kuch bhi likhne ki zarurat nahi

---

# ❌ Kisi Ek Page Par Layout Nahi Chahiye To?

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar kisi ek view par layout nahi chahiye?

**Student:**
Us view ke top par likho:

```cshtml
@{
    Layout = null;
}
```

---

# 🎭 Multiple Layout Pages Concept

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ek project me multiple layouts kab chahiye?

**Student:**
Examples:

* **Before Login** → Normal layout
* **After Login / Dashboard** → Different layout

---

## ❓ Teacher: Multiple layout kaise use karte hain?

**Student:**

1. Shared folder me **dusra layout** banao

   * `_MyLayout.cshtml`

2. Specific view me likho:

```cshtml
@{
    Layout = "_MyLayout";
}
```

👉 Ye **_ViewStart** wale layout ko override kar dega.

---

# 🧠 Final Summary (Interview + Practical Ready)

---

### ✅ Is video me humne kya seekha?

1. **Bootstrap Navbar** layout me kaise add kare
2. **Bootstrap Carousel** with auto sliding
3. **Images use karna** using:

   * `wwwroot`
   * `UseStaticFiles()`
   * `<base>` tag
4. **_ViewStart.cshtml** se layout auto apply
5. **Layout override / multiple layouts**

---

**Student:**
👉 **RenderBody vs RenderSection**
👉 **Sections in Layout**
👉 Advanced layout concepts

---

Agar chaho to next step me main:

* **Real interview questions on Layout**

bhi bana deta hoon 👍

# 📘 Introduction to Entity Framework Core (EF Core)

*(ASP.NET Core MVC – Part 31)*

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Hum ASP.NET Core MVC kyun padh rahe hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, hum **ASP.NET Core MVC** isliye padh rahe hain taaki hum **Web Applications** bana sakein.

👉 **Web Application** ka matlab:

* User se **data collect** kar sake
* Data ko **process** kar sake
* Data ko **store** kar sake
* Aur jab zarurat ho, **wapas provide** kar sake

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Simple HTML, CSS, JavaScript se website ban jaati hai, phir itna framework kyun?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, simple website sirf **static** hoti hai
Lekin **Web Application**:

* Login
* Registration
* Forms
* Reports
* Business logic

jaise kaam karti hai, isliye framework chahiye.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Abhi tak hum data kahan store kar rahe the?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, abhi tak hum:

* **List**
* **Objects**

use kar rahe the, jo **memory (RAM)** me hote hain.

👉 Problem:

* Application band → **data lost**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Permanent data store karna ho to kya use karte hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Database** use karte hain.

Examples:

* **SQL Server**
* **Oracle**
* **MySQL**
* **MongoDB**

👉 Large data, fast access, update/delete ke liye **Database best option** hai.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

.NET Application aur Database alag-alag technologies hain, connect kaise hoti hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, dono ke beech ek **bridge (पुल)** chahiye.

👉 Ye bridge:

* Application ↔ Database connect karta hai
* Data ka **exchange** karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Pehle kaunsa bridge use hota tha?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, pehle **ADO.NET** use hota tha.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ADO.NET me problem kya thi?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, ADO.NET me:

* **SQL queries manually likhni padti thi**
* Queries **database specific** hoti thi

👉 Example:

* SQL Server ke liye alag code
* Oracle ke liye alag code
* MySQL ke liye alag code

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Database change ho jaye to kya problem hoti thi?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, agar database change ho gaya:

* Poora **database code rewrite** karna padta tha
* Bahut **time, effort, cost** lagta tha

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Is problem ka solution kya nikla?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, is problem ke solution ke liye **Entity Framework** aaya.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Entity Framework kya hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Entity Framework (EF Core)** ek **ORM – Object Relational Mapping framework** hai.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ORM ka matlab kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **ORM** ka matlab:

👉 Database ka **Table**
↔
👉 Application ka **Object / Class**

Matlab:

* Object me data dalo → Table me jayega
* Object se data lo → Table se aayega

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core ki definition batao.

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir,

**Entity Framework Core** is:

* **Open Source**
* **Lightweight**
* **Cross Platform**
* **ORM Framework**
  for **.NET Applications**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Open Source ka matlab?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Free hai
* License kharidne ki zarurat nahi
* Internet se **packages install** karke use kar sakte hain

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Lightweight ka matlab?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Koi heavy software install nahi
* Sirf kuch **NuGet packages** install karne hote hain

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Cross Platform ka matlab?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Windows
* Linux
* macOS

👉 Har OS par kaam karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core ka main kaam kya hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, EF Core:

* **Database Access Technology** hai
* .NET Application aur Database ke beech **bridge** hai
* SQL likhne ki jagah **Objects ke saath kaam** karwata hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core SQL queries likhne se kaise bachata hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* EF Core **LINQ queries** use karta hai
* Developer **C# Objects** pe kaam karta hai
* EF Core khud SQL generate karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core ADO.NET se kaise better hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* ADO.NET me **manual SQL**
* EF Core me **automatic SQL**
* EF Core me:

  * Insert
  * Update
  * Delete
    sab **automatic** hota hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core purana hai ya naya?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Pehle **Entity Framework**
* Phir **EF 6**
* Ab **EF Core (Redesigned)**

👉 EF Core:

* Cross-platform
* Open source
* Modern & fast

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core ka real-life use example?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Registration Form** → Data database me save
* **Login Form** → ID/password database se check
* **Student / Employee Management**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core ke main features kya hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

### 🔹 1. **Object Relational Mapping (ORM)**

* Class ↔ Table
* Object ↔ Row

---

### 🔹 2. **Cross Platform Support**

* Windows, Linux, macOS

---

### 🔹 3. **Two Approaches**

#### ✅ **Code First**

* Pehle **C# Classes**
* Database & Tables **auto create**

#### ✅ **Database First**

* Pehle **Database**
* EF Core **classes auto generate**

---

### 🔹 4. **LINQ Queries**

* SQL ki jagah **LINQ**
* Easy & readable

---

### 🔹 5. **Change Tracking**

* Object me change → EF Core track karta hai
* Save karte hi database update

---

### 🔹 6. **Migrations**

* Database changes ka **version control**
* Easy rollback possible

---

### 🔹 7. **Multiple Database Providers**

* SQL Server
* Oracle
* MySQL
* PostgreSQL

👉 Sirf **provider & connection string change**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core kyun seekhna zaruri hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* ASP.NET Core app ko database se connect karna ho
* Clean & maintainable code chahiye
* Database change future me possible ho

👉 **EF Core must-learn hai**

---

## 🔑 One-Line Summary (Exam / Interview)

👉 **Entity Framework Core ek ORM framework hai jo .NET application aur database ke beech bridge ka kaam karta hai aur SQL likhe bina database operations karne deta hai.**

---

Agar chaho to next mai bana sakta hoon:

✅ EF Core Architecture diagram
✅ Code First practical example
✅ Database First demo
✅ EF Core Interview Q&A
✅ EF Core CRUD with ASP.NET Core MVC

Bas bolo 👍

Ye notes **Entity Framework Core – Introduction Part 2 (ASP.NET Core MVC)** video par based hain.

# 📘 Entity Framework Core – Introduction (Part 2)

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Last session me humne kya padha tha?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, last session me humne padha tha:

* **Entity Framework Core kya hai**
* Ye **web application ko database se connect** karta hai
* Data ko **insert, update, delete, fetch** karne me help karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Entity Framework Core ka base concept kya hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Entity Framework Core pura ka pura ORM pe based hai**.

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ORM ka full form kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **ORM = Object Relational Mapping**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Object Relational Mapping ka matlab kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **ORM ka matlab**:

* **Object** (C# class ka object)
* **Relational** (Database table)

👉 Object aur Table ke beech **mapping (linking)**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ORM me actual kaam kaise hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Object me data dalo → **Database table me insert**
* Object se data lo → **Database se fetch**
* Object update karo → **Database update**
* Object delete karo → **Database se delete**

👉 Developer object pe kaam karta hai, kaam database me hota hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ORM Entity Framework Core ke liye itna important kyun hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **ORM hi Entity Framework Core ka foundation hai**
Bina ORM ke EF Core exist hi nahi karta

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Domain Object kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Domain** ka matlab hota hai:
👉 Kis field ke liye application ban rahi hai

### Examples:

* Hospital software → **Hospital domain**
* Bank application → **Bank domain**
* School software → **School domain**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Domain Object ka example do.

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, agar **Hospital domain** hai to:

* Doctor
* Patient
* Prescription
* Medicine
* OPD / IPD

👉 Ye sab **Domain Objects** hain

Agar **School domain** hai to:

* School
* Class
* Teacher
* Student
* Attendance
* Fees
* Marks

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Domain Object ka relation database se kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Har Domain Object ke liye database me ek table**
* Ya
* **Har table ke liye application me ek object**

👉 EF Core in dono ko **map** karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

ORM ka main kaam kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Object se data database me store karna
* Database se data object me lana
* Ye sab process **simplify** karna

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core me database se connect karne ke kitne approaches hote hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **2 main approaches** hote hain:

1️⃣ **Code First**
2️⃣ **Database First**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Code First approach kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Code First approach** me:

* Pehle **C# Classes (Domain Objects)** bante hain
* EF Core automatically:

  * Database
  * Tables
  * Columns
    generate kar deta hai

👉 Database ka SQL likhne ki zarurat nahi

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Code First kab use karna chahiye?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Agar **Object Oriented Programming strong** hai
* Database design me utna confidence nahi
* New project start kar rahe ho

👉 **Code First best hai**

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Database First approach kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Database First approach** me:

* Pehle **Database aur Tables already bane hote hain**
* EF Core un tables ke base pe:

  * Classes
  * DbContext
    automatically generate karta hai (Scaffolding)

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Database First kab use hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Agar **database pehle se exist** karta hai
* Purana system migrate kar rahe ho (PHP → ASP.NET)
* Data loss nahi karna

👉 Wahan **Database First compulsory** hota hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Code First aur Database First me kaunsa better hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **dono hi ache hain** ❌ koi bad nahi

👉 Depend karta hai:

* Developer ki **skills**
* Project ki **requirement**
* Database pehle se hai ya nahi

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core me Database Provider kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Database Provider** ek component hota hai jo:

* EF Core aur Database ke beech **translator** ka kaam karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Translator kyun chahiye?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* C# ek language hai
* SQL ek alag language hai

👉 Dono ek-dusre ko directly nahi samajh sakte
👉 Beech me **Database Provider** translation karta hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Database Provider ka kaam kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* LINQ ko SQL me convert karna
* Query execute karna
* Transaction handle karna
* Migration support dena

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Configuration ka role kya hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Database ka **server name**
* Database ka **name**
* **Username / Password**
* Connection settings

👉 Ye sab configuration ke through diya jata hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

EF Core me LINQ ka use kyun hota hai?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* SQL likhne ke bajay **LINQ queries**
* Data filter, search, join kar sakte hain
* Code readable & safe hota hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Kuch popular database providers ke naam batao.

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

* **SQL Server**
  👉 `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer`

* **MySQL**
  👉 `Pomelo.EntityFrameworkCore.MySql`

* **Oracle**
  👉 `Oracle.EntityFrameworkCore`

* **PostgreSQL**
  👉 `Npgsql.EntityFrameworkCore.PostgreSQL`

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Database change ho jaye to code me kya change karna padega?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Business code same rahega**
* Sirf:

  * Database Provider
  * Connection String
    change karni padegi

👉 Ye EF Core ka **biggest advantage** hai

---

## 🧑‍🏫 Teacher:

Next kya padhne wale hain?

## 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Pehle **Database First Approach** practical
* Basic configuration steps
* Real example ke saath database connect karna
* Baad me **Code First Approach**

---

## 🔑 One-Line Summary (Exam / Interview)

👉 **Entity Framework Core ORM-based framework hai jo object ke through database operations perform karne deta hai aur Code First & Database First dono approaches support karta hai.**

---

Agar chaho to next mai bana sakta hoon:

✅ Database First – Step by Step Practical
✅ EF Core DbContext & DbSet deep explanation
✅ EF Core Interview Questions
✅ Code First with Migrations
✅ Real ASP.NET Core MVC CRUD example

Below are **Q&A-style notes** for **Part 32: Introduction to Entity Framework Core (ASP.NET Core MVC – Part 2)**.
Language: **Simple Hindi + English (Hinglish)**
Style: **Teacher asks → Student answers**, beginner-friendly, step-by-step, with examples.

---

## 🎓 Topic: **Entity Framework Core – Part 2 (Concepts & Approaches)**

---

### ❓ Teacher: Entity Framework Core kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Entity Framework Core (EF Core)** ek **framework** hai jo **ASP.NET Core application** ko **database ke saath interact** karne me help karta hai.
Iske through hum:

* Data **insert**
* Data **read**
* Data **update**
* Data **delete**
  sab kuch **C# objects** ke through kar sakte hain, bina direct SQL likhe.

---

### ❓ Teacher: EF Core ki need kyu padi? Traditional ADO.NET me kya problem thi?

**Student:**
Traditional **ADO.NET** me:

* Bahut **manual SQL queries** likhni padti thi
* Zyada **boilerplate code** hota tha
* Maintenance difficult hoti thi

In problems ko solve karne ke liye **Entity Framework** aaya.

---

### ❓ Teacher: EF Core kis concept pe based hai?

**Student:**
EF Core poora ka poora **ORM** pe based hai.

👉 **ORM = Object Relational Mapping**

---

### ❓ Teacher: ORM kya hota hai? (Interview Question 🔥)

**Student:**
**ORM (Object Relational Mapping)** ka matlab hota hai:

* **Object** ko **Database Table** ke saath map karna

Matlab:

* Object me data add → Database me insert
* Object se data read → Database se select
* Object update → Database update
* Object delete → Database delete

Hum kaam **objects** pe karte hain, but effect **database** pe hota hai.

---

### ❓ Teacher: Object aur Relation ka matlab yahan kya hai?

**Student:**

* **Object** → C# class ka instance (memory me hota hai)
* **Relation** → Database ki **tables**

ORM in dono ke beech **mapping** karta hai.

---

### ❓ Teacher: Domain kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Domain** ka matlab hota hai **application ka area** jiske liye software ban raha hai.

### 🔹 Examples:

* Hospital software → **Hospital domain**
* Bank software → **Bank domain**
* School software → **School domain**

---

### ❓ Teacher: Domain Object kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Domain ke andar jo entities hoti hain unko **Domain Objects** kehte hain.

### 🔹 Hospital Domain Example:

* Doctor
* Patient
* Prescription
* OPD
* IPD
* Medicine

👉 Ye sab **Domain Objects / Model Classes** hote hain.

---

### ❓ Teacher: EF Core domain objects ke saath kya karta hai?

**Student:**
EF Core:

* Har **domain object** ko
* Ek **database table** ke saath map karta hai

👉 Object ↔ Table

---

### ❓ Teacher: ORM ka main kaam kya hai?

**Student:**

* Application se data ko **easily database me store** karna
* Database se data ko **easily read** karna
* SQL complexity ko **hide** karna

Isliye bola jata hai:

> **ORM simplifies data access**

---

## 🛠️ EF Core Development Approaches

---

### ❓ Teacher: EF Core me database ke saath kaam karne ke kitne approaches hote hain?

**Student:**
EF Core me **2 main approaches** hote hain:

1. **Code First Approach**
2. **Database First Approach**

---

### ❓ Teacher: Code First Approach kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Code First** me:

* Pehle **C# classes (domain objects)** banate hain
* EF Core un classes se **database & tables auto-generate** karta hai

👉 SQL likhne ki tension nahi.

### 🔹 Kab use kare?

* Jab **OOP (C#)** strong ho
* Database design me confidence kam ho

---

### ❓ Teacher: Code First ka simple example?

**Student:**

```csharp
class Student {
    public int Id { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
}
```

👉 EF Core automatically:

* **Student table**
* Id, Name columns
  create kar dega.

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database First Approach kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Database First** me:

* Pehle **database & tables** banate hain
* EF Core un tables se **classes auto-generate** karta hai
  (is process ko **Scaffolding** bolte hain)

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database First kab use karte hain?

**Student:**

* Jab **database already exist** karta ho
* Jab purana system kisi aur technology (PHP, etc.) me ho
* Jab database me **existing data** ho

👉 Database ko change nahi kar sakte, isliye **Database First** use hota hai.

---

### ❓ Teacher: Code First aur Database First – kaunsa better hai?

**Student:**
👉 **Dono ache hain**, depend karta hai:

* Aapki **skills** par
* Project ki **requirement** par

❌ Koi bhi approach bad ya good nahi hota.

---

## 🔌 Database Provider Concept

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database Provider kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Database Provider** ek **bridge / translator** hota hai jo:

* C# code ko
* Database language (SQL, Oracle, etc.) me convert karta hai

Aur:

* Database se aane wale data ko
* Wapas C# objects me convert karta hai

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database Provider ki need kyu padti hai?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* **C#** aur **SQL** alag-alag languages hain
* Direct communication possible nahi

Isliye beech me **provider** chahiye.

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database Provider kya-kya kaam karta hai?

**Student:**
Database Provider:

* Queries execute karta hai
* Transactions manage karta hai
* Migrations handle karta hai
* LINQ ko SQL me convert karta hai

---

### ❓ Teacher: Database Provider configuration kaha hoti hai?

**Student:**

* **Startup / Program.cs** me
* **DbContextOptions** ke through

Yahin pe:

* Server name
* Database name
* Username
* Password
  sab configure hota hai.

---

### ❓ Teacher: EF Core LINQ kaise use karta hai?

**Student:**
EF Core **LINQ (Language Integrated Query)** support karta hai.

Matlab:

* SQL likhne ki jagah
* C# queries likhte hain

### 🔹 Example:

```csharp
var students = context.Students.Where(s => s.Name == "Amit");
```

👉 EF Core isko SQL me convert kar deta hai.

---

## 📦 Popular Database Providers

---

### ❓ Teacher: SQL Server ke liye kaunsa provider use hota hai?

**Student:**
👉 `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer`

---

### ❓ Teacher: MySQL ke liye?

**Student:**
👉 `Pomelo.EntityFrameworkCore.MySql`

---

### ❓ Teacher: Oracle ke liye?

**Student:**
👉 `Oracle.EntityFrameworkCore`

---

### ❓ Teacher: PostgreSQL ke liye?

**Student:**
👉 `Npgsql.EntityFrameworkCore.PostgreSQL`

---

### ❓ Teacher: Agar future me database change ho jaye to?

**Student:**
EF Core ka fayda ye hai ki:

* **Application code same rahega**
* Sirf **Database Provider change** karna padega

👉 Major code changes nahi chahiye 👍

---

## 📌 Summary (Quick Revision)

* **EF Core** → Database interaction easy banata hai
* **ORM** → Object ↔ Table mapping
* **Domain Object** → Real-world entities
* **Approaches** → Code First & Database First
* **Database Provider** → C# aur DB ke beech bridge
* **LINQ** → SQL ke bina querying

---

**Student:**
Next part me:

* **Database First Approach** ka
* **Practical implementation & steps** dekhenge

---

* **Short revision notes**
* **Interview Q&A**
* **Diagram-based explanation**
* **Code First vs Database First table**

bhi bana deta hoon 🙂

# 📘 Part 33 – DB First Approach (EF Core)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Abhi tak hum Entity Framework Core me kya-kya padh chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, abhi tak humne ye topics cover kiye hain:

1. **Entity Framework Core kya hai**
2. **ORM (Object Relational Mapping)**
3. **Code First & Database First Approach**
4. **Database Providers**

Ab aaj hum **implementation** start kar rahe hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka main topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Aaj ka topic hai **Database First Approach ka practical implementation** in **ASP.NET Core MVC**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database First Approach kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Database First Approach** ek development method hai jisme:

* **Pehle database design hota hai**
* Tables, columns, data pehle ban jata hai
* Uske baad **Entity Framework Core automatically classes generate karta hai**

👉 Matlab:
**Database → Code (Models + DbContext)**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database First kab use karte hain?

**Student:**
Database First use karte hain jab:

* Database **already exist** karta ho
* SQL Server pehle se bana ho
* Project me **existing data** ho
* Migration / new technology me shift kar rahe ho

---

# 🪜 Step-by-Step Implementation (DB First)

---

## ✅ STEP 1: Database Create karna (SQL Server)

### ❓ Teacher: Sabse pehla step kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sabse pehle **database banana** padta hai.

### 🔹 Example:

```sql
CREATE DATABASE TestDB
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database me table banana kyu zaroori hai?

**Student:**
Kyuki **EF Core me har table ke liye ek Primary Key mandatory hoti hai**, warna EF kaam nahi karega.

---

### 🔹 Student Table Example:

```sql
CREATE TABLE Student (
    Roll INT CONSTRAINT PK_Roll PRIMARY KEY,
    Name VARCHAR(100),
    Gender VARCHAR(50),
    DOB DATE,
    Phone VARCHAR(10)
)
```

---

### ❓ Teacher: Table me data bhi dalna zaroori hai?

**Student:**
Haan sir, taaki baad me **read (select)** operation dikha sake.

```sql
INSERT INTO Student VALUES
(1,'Akash Kumar','Male','2003-09-12','9876543210')
```

---

## ✅ STEP 2: ASP.NET Core MVC Project Create karna

### ❓ Teacher: Database ban gaya, ab next kya?

**Student:**
Ab **Visual Studio me new ASP.NET Core MVC project** banana hai.

Example Project Name:
👉 **DBFirstDemo**

⚠️ Important:

* **.NET 9.0** use karna hai (packages latest version ke liye)

---

## ✅ STEP 3: Required NuGet Packages Install karna

### ❓ Teacher: Database First ke liye kitne packages chahiye?

**Student:**
Sir, **3 mandatory packages** install karne padte hain:

### 📦 Required Packages:

1. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer**
   👉 SQL Server ke liye provider

2. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Tools**
   👉 Scaffold command chalane ke liye

3. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design**
   👉 Code scaffolding & templates ke liye

---

### ❓ Teacher: Packages kaha se install karte hain?

**Student:**
Visual Studio me:

👉 **Tools → NuGet Package Manager → Manage NuGet Packages for Solution → Browse**

---

## ✅ STEP 4: Verify Packages

### ❓ Teacher: Kaise confirm karein ki packages install ho gaye?

**Student:**
Solution Explorer → **Dependencies → Packages**

Agar ye teen dikh rahe hain, matlab installation successful hai ✔️

---

## ✅ STEP 5: Reverse Engineering (Scaffolding)

---

### ❓ Teacher: Reverse Engineering kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Reverse Engineering ka matlab:

👉 **Database se code generate karna**

* Tables → Model Classes
* Database → **DbContext**

---

### ❓ Teacher: Reverse Engineering kaise hoti hai?

**Student:**
Iske liye **Scaffold-DbContext command** run karni padti hai.

---

### 🖥️ Package Manager Console open karo:

👉 **Tools → NuGet Package Manager → Package Manager Console**

---

### 🔑 Connection String kya hoti hai?

**Student:**
Connection String batati hai:

* Server ka naam
* Database ka naam
* Authentication type

---

### 🔹 Windows Authentication Example:

```text
Server=DESKTOP-XXXX;
Database=TestDB;
Trusted_Connection=True;
TrustServerCertificate=True;
```

---

### 🔹 SQL Authentication Example:

```text
Server=DESKTOP-XXXX;
Database=TestDB;
User Id=sa;
Password=pass123;
TrustServerCertificate=True;
```

---

### 🧾 Scaffold Command (Full Example):

```powershell
Scaffold-DbContext
"Server=DESKTOP-XXXX;Database=TestDB;Trusted_Connection=True;TrustServerCertificate=True"
Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer
-OutputDir Models
```

---

### ❓ Teacher: Command successful hui ya nahi kaise pata chalega?

**Student:**
Agar:

* **Red error nahi aaya**
* Command prompt wapas aa gaya

👉 Matlab scaffolding successful 🎉

---

## ✅ STEP 6: Generated Files Samajhna

---

### ❓ Teacher: Scaffold ke baad kya-kya generate hota hai?

**Student:**

1. **Model Class** (e.g. `Student.cs`)

   * Har column → ek property

2. **DbContext Class** (e.g. `TestDBContext.cs`)

   * Database ka main controller
   * Tables ko represent karta hai via **DbSet**

---

### 🔹 Example:

```csharp
public virtual DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; }
```

👉 Ye **Student table** ko represent karta hai.

---

## ✅ STEP 7: Connection String ko DbContext se hatao

---

### ❓ Teacher: DbContext se connection string kyu hatate hain?

**Student:**
Agar connection string DbContext me rahegi to:

* Server change hone par **code change** karna padega
* Ye **bad practice** hai

Isliye hum:
👉 **DbContext → appsettings.json** me shift karte hain

---

### 🔥 DbContext me kya delete karna hai?

**Student:**
Method dhundho:

```csharp
protected override void OnConfiguring(...)
```

Iske andar ki **2 lines delete** kar do
Aur sirf empty method chhod do.

---

## ✅ STEP 8: appsettings.json me Connection String add karo

---

### ❓ Teacher: Connection string kaha likhte hain?

**Student:**
`appsettings.json` me:

```json
"ConnectionStrings": {
  "ConStr": "Server=DESKTOP-XXXX;Database=TestDB;Trusted_Connection=True;TrustServerCertificate=True"
}
```

👉 **ConStr** ek name hai (aage use hoga)

---

## ✅ STEP 9: DbContext ko Program.cs me register karo

---

### ❓ Teacher: Ye step kyu zaroori hai?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* DbContext ko **application ke services me add** karna hota hai
* Taki controllers database se baat kar sakein

---

### ✔️ Best & Easy Way (Single Line):

```csharp
builder.Services.AddDbContext<TestDBContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlServer(
        builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("ConStr")));
```

---

### ❓ Teacher: Is line me kya ho raha hai?

**Student:**

* **AddDbContext** → DbContext ko service bana rahe hain
* **UseSqlServer** → SQL Server use hoga
* **GetConnectionString("ConStr")** → appsettings.json se value uth rahi hai

---

## 🧠 Final Summary (Yaad Rakhne Layak)

### 🔑 DB First Setup Steps:

1. **Database & Tables banao**
2. **ASP.NET Core MVC project create karo**
3. **3 NuGet packages install karo**
4. **Scaffold-DbContext command run karo**
5. **Connection string DbContext se hatao**
6. **appsettings.json me daalo**
7. **Program.cs me DbContext register karo**

👉 Ye sab **sirf ek baar** karna padta hai.

Using **Database First + EF Core**

# 📘 Part 34 – CRUD (READ Operation)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Abhi tak hum Entity Framework Core me kya-kya setup kar chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, abhi tak humne:

* **EF Core ka basic setup**
* **Database First approach**
* **DbContext generate karna**
* **Connection string configuration**
* **Program.cs me DbContext register karna**

ye sab **one-time setup** kar liya hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: CRUD ka full form kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**CRUD** ka matlab hota hai:

* **C – Create** (Insert)
* **R – Read** (Data dekhna)
* **U – Update**
* **D – Delete**

Aaj hum **READ operation** padhenge.

---

## ❓ Teacher: READ operation me kya-kya aata hai?

**Student:**
READ operation me **2 parts** hote hain:

1. **List** – saare records ek saath dikhana
2. **Details** – ek particular record ka full detail (profile)

---

# 🔑 DbContext – Core Concept

---

## ❓ Teacher: DbContext itna important kyu hota hai?

**Student:**
**DbContext** EF Core ka **central component** hota hai.

👉 Ye **bridge** ka kaam karta hai:

* **.NET Application ↔ Database**

---

## ❓ Teacher: DbContext kya-kya handle karta hai?

**Student:**

DbContext handle karta hai:

* **Database connection info** (server, DB name, auth)
* **Data retrieval** (SELECT)
* **Data manipulation** (INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE)
* **Transaction management**
* **Change tracking**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Model class aur DbSet ka role kya hai?

**Student:**

* **Model class** → Table ke **ek record** ko represent karti hai
* **DbSet** → Table ke **multiple records** ko represent karta hai

Example:

```csharp
public DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; }
```

👉 Ye **Student table** ko represent karta hai.

---

# 🧱 Controller me DbContext ka Setup

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database se data read karne ke liye code kaha likhte hain?

**Student:**
Sir, **Controller** ke andar.

Kyuki:

* Saare **database operations**
* **Action methods** ke through hote hain

---

## ❓ Teacher: Controller me DbContext kaise use karte hain?

**Student:**
Controller ke andar:

### ✅ Step 1: DbContext ka reference banao

```csharp
private readonly TestDBContext context;
```

---

### ✅ Step 2: Constructor me inject karo

```csharp
public HomeController(TestDBContext context)
{
    this.context = context;
}
```

👉 Isko bolte hain **Dependency Injection**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar controller me constructor pehle se na ho to?

**Student:**

2 tareeke hain:

### 🔹 Manually:

```csharp
public StudentController(TestDBContext context)
{
    this.context = context;
}
```

### 🔹 Automatically:

* DbContext field select karo
* 🔧 (light bulb) → **Generate Constructor**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kya har controller me DbContext chahiye?

**Student:**
❌ Nahi sir.

👉 Sirf **us controller me** chahiye
jaha **database se kaam** ho raha ho.

---

# 📖 READ – LIST Operation

---

## ❓ Teacher: LIST operation ka goal kya hai?

**Student:**
Database ke **saare records** ko:

* read karna
* aur **table format me view me dikhana**

---

## ❓ Teacher: LIST ke liye kaunsa action method banega?

**Student:**

```csharp
public IActionResult List()
{
    List<Student> students = context.Students.ToList();
    return View(students);
}
```

---

### 🧠 Explanation:

* `context.Students` → Student table
* `ToList()` → saare records list me
* `View(students)` → View ko data pass

---

## ❓ Teacher: LIST ka View kaise banate hain?

**Student:**

### ✅ Scaffold View use karte hain:

* Action method par **Right click**
* **Add View**
* Template: **List**
* Model: **Student**
* DbContext: **TestDBContext**

👉 EF Core **automatically HTML table generate** kar deta hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: List View me kya-kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* `<table>`
* `<thead>` → column headings
* `<tbody>` → data rows
* `foreach` loop → multiple records
* Default buttons:

  * **Edit**
  * **Details**
  * **Delete**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Extra column (Roll Number) kaise add karein?

**Student:**

* Table header me `<th>`
* Loop ke andar `<td>`

Manually add karke **customize** kar sakte ho.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Column ka naam change kaise karein?

**Student:**

Model class me **Display attribute** use karke:

```csharp
[Display(Name = "Roll Number")]
public int Roll { get; set; }
```

👉 `@Html.DisplayNameFor` automatically new name use karega.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Buttons ko attractive kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Bootstrap classes use karke:

```html
class="btn btn-success"
class="btn btn-primary"
class="btn btn-warning"
class="btn btn-danger"
```

---

# 📄 READ – DETAILS Operation

---

## ❓ Teacher: DETAILS operation ka use kya hai?

**Student:**
Ek **specific record** ka:

* complete detail
* profile-type page

---

## ❓ Teacher: DETAILS action method kaise banega?

**Student:**

```csharp
public IActionResult Details(int? id)
{
    if (id == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Please pass roll number";
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    Student st = context.Students
        .FirstOrDefault(x => x.Roll == id);

    if (st == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Record not found for roll " + id;
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    return View(st);
}
```

---

### 🧠 Explanation:

* `int? id` → nullable (ID aaye ya na aaye)
* `FirstOrDefault` → ek record search
* `TempData` → message pass karne ke liye
* `RedirectToAction` → List page pe bhejne ke liye

---

## ❓ Teacher: TempData kyun use kiya?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* Data **ek action se doosre action** me bhejna tha
* ViewModel use nahi kar rahe

👉 **TempData** is best for redirection messages.

---

## ❓ Teacher: TempData ko View me kaise show karein?

**Student:**

```razor
@if (TempData["Message"] != null)
{
    <div class="alert alert-danger">
        @TempData["Message"]
    </div>
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Alert ko professional kaise banayein?

**Student:**
Bootstrap ka **dismissible alert** use karke
(close ❌ button ke saath).

---

## ❓ Teacher: DETAILS ka View kaise banate hain?

**Student:**

* Action method par **Right click**
* **Add View**
* Template: **Details**
* Model: **Student**
* DbContext select karo

👉 Profile-type layout auto generate ho jata hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Details View me Roll Number kaise dikhayein?

**Student:**

* `<dt>` → Label
* `<dd>` → Data

Manually add karke Roll show kar sakte ho.

---

## ❓ Teacher: “Back to List” link galat page pe ja raha hai, kya karein?

**Student:**

Index ke jagah:

```razor
asp-action="List"
```

set kar do.

---

# 🎨 Basic UI Customization

---

## ❓ Teacher: CSS kaha likhte hain?

**Student:**
👉 `wwwroot/css/site.css`

Example:

```css
.tt {
    color: blue;
    border: 2px dashed red;
}
```

Aur class View me apply kar do.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Bootstrap se layout kaise improve karein?

**Student:**

* `bg-light`
* `bg-info`
* `p-3`, `p-5`
* `text-white`

Use karke profile page ko clean bana sakte ho.

# 📘 Part 35 – CRUD (CREATE / INSERT Operation)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pichhle session me humne kya complete kiya tha?

**Student:**
Sir, pichhle session me humne **READ operation** complete kiya tha, jisme:

* **List** – saare records dikhaye
* **Details** – ek specific record ka detail (profile view)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Aaj ka topic kya hai?

**Student:**
Aaj hum **CREATE operation** padhenge.
Matlab:

👉 **Form open ho**
👉 **User data bhare**
👉 **Submit kare**
👉 **Data database me INSERT ho**

---

## ❓ Teacher: CREATE operation ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
CREATE ka matlab hota hai:

* **New record insert karna**
* Database me **naya data add karna**

CRUD me isko bolte hain **C – Create**

---

# 🧱 Step 1: CREATE Action Method (GET)

---

## ❓ Teacher: CREATE pe click karne par kya hota hai?

**Student:**
CREATE pe click karne par **Create action method** call hota hai.

Isliye sabse pehle **Create action method** banana padega.

---

### ✅ Controller me (GET method):

```csharp
public IActionResult Create()
{
    return View();
}
```

👉 Ye sirf **form dikhane** ke liye hota hai
👉 By default ye **GET request** hoti hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form ka View kaise banega?

**Student:**
Hum **Scaffolding** use karenge, taaki form auto-generate ho jaye.

---

### 🧾 Steps:

## ❓ Teacher: Isse kya milega?

# 🎨 Small UI Improvements (Optional but Important)

## ❓ Teacher: Date of Birth ke liye calendar kaise laate hain?

```html
<input type="date" />
```

# 🧠 Important Concept: GET vs POST

---

## ❓ Teacher: CREATE ke liye 2 action method kyu chahiye?

**Student:**

CREATE ke liye **2 kaam** hote hain:

1. **Form dikhana** → GET request
2. **Form ka data process karna** → POST request

Isliye **same naam ke 2 action methods** bante hain.

---

# 🧱 Step 2: CREATE Action Method (POST)

---

## ❓ Teacher: POST method kaise banate hain?

**Student:**

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Create(Student st)
{
    // logic yahan aayega
}
```

👉 Yahan `Student st` automatically form ka data le lega
👉 Isko bolte hain **Model Binding**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form ka data Student object me kaise aata hai?

**Student:**
Form ke input fields ke **name** model ke **property names** se match karte hain.

Example:

* Input → `Roll`
* Model property → `Roll`

MVC automatically values fill kar deta hai.

---

# ✅ Validation (Required & Length)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation kyu lagate hain?

**Student:**
Taaki:

* Empty data na aaye
* Galat length ka phone number na aaye
* User ko proper error message mile

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation kaise lagate hain?

**Student:**
Model class (`Student.cs`) me **Data Annotations** use karte hain:

```csharp
[Required]
public int Roll { get; set; }

[Required]
public string Name { get; set; }

[Required]
[StringLength(13, MinimumLength = 10)]
public string Phone { get; set; }
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation check kaise hoti hai controller me?

**Student:**

```csharp
if (ModelState.IsValid)
{
    // Insert logic
}
else
{
    return View(st);
}
```

👉 Agar validation fail hui:

* Same form wapas aayega
* Error messages dikhenge
* Bhara hua data gayab nahi hoga

---

# 🗄️ Step 3: Database me Data INSERT karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Data database me kaise insert hota hai?

**Student:**

### Step 1: DbSet me add karo

```csharp
context.Students.Add(st);
```

### Step 2: Database me save karo

```csharp
context.SaveChanges();
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Sirf Add karne se data DB me kyu nahi jata?

**Student:**
Kyuki `Add()` sirf **RAM (memory)** me add karta hai.

Database update ke liye:
👉 **SaveChanges() mandatory hai**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Insert ke baad user ko kaha bhejte hain?

**Student:**
Insert ke baad user ko **List page** pe redirect karte hain:

```csharp
return RedirectToAction("List");
```

---

# 🟢 Success Message dikhana

---

## ❓ Teacher: “Data Successfully Inserted” message kaise dikhayein?

**Student:**
Hum **TempData** use karte hain.

---

### Controller me:

```csharp
TempData["Success"] = "Data successfully inserted";
```

---

### List View me:

```razor
@if (TempData["Success"] != null)
{
    <div class="alert alert-success">
        @TempData["Success"]
    </div>
}
```

---

# ❌ Exception Handling (Try–Catch)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Duplicate Roll Number dalne par kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Exception aata hai kyuki:

* Roll Number **Primary Key** hai
* Duplicate allowed nahi hota

---

## ❓ Teacher: Is exception ko handle kaise karein?

**Student:**
**Try–Catch** use karte hain:

```csharp
try
{
    context.Students.Add(st);
    context.SaveChanges();
    TempData["Success"] = "Data successfully inserted";
}
catch
{
    TempData["Message"] = "Unable to insert data. Please check.";
}
return RedirectToAction("List");
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Try–Catch ka fayda kya hai?

**Student:**

* Application **crash nahi hoti**
* User ko **simple message** milta hai
* Technical error hide ho jata hai

---

# ✅ Final CREATE Action (Complete Logic)

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Create(Student st)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        try
        {
            context.Students.Add(st);
            context.SaveChanges();
            TempData["Success"] = "Data successfully inserted";
            return RedirectToAction("List");
        }
        catch
        {
            TempData["Message"] = "Unable to insert data. Please check.";
            return RedirectToAction("List");
        }
    }
    return View(st);
}
```

# 📘 Part 36 – CRUD (UPDATE Operation)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab tak hum CRUD me kya-kya complete kar chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, Database First Approach me humne:

* **CREATE (Insert)** ✔
* **READ (List & Details)** ✔
  Complete kar liya hai.

Aaj hum **UPDATE** padhenge.

---

## ❓ Teacher: UPDATE operation ka real-life example kya ho sakta hai?

**Student:**
Sir, jaise:

* Student ka **mobile number change** ho gaya
* **Name spelling galat** insert ho gaya
* **Gender / DOB** galat save ho gaya

Toh database me existing data ko **change karna = UPDATE**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: UPDATE form ka behaviour CREATE se kaise different hota hai?

**Student:**

* CREATE me **empty form** hota hai
* UPDATE me **same form hota hai**,
  👉 **but data pehle se filled hota hai**

User sirf changes karta hai aur **Update** pe click karta hai.

---

# 🧱 Step 1: Edit Button ka Flow Samajhna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit button pe click karne par kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Edit button:

* **Edit action method** ko call karta hai
* **Roll Number (Primary Key)** ko parameter ke form me pass karta hai

Example URL:

```
/Home/Edit/2
```

Yahan **2 = Roll Number (ID)**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Roll number har jagah kyu pass hota hai (Edit, Details, Delete)?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* **Primary Key** ke base par hi record identify hota hai
* Update / Delete / Details sab **same ID** se kaam karte hain

---

# 🧱 Step 2: Edit – GET Action Method

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form dikhane ke liye kya banana padega?

**Student:**
Ek **GET Edit Action Method** banana padega.

---

### ✅ Controller Code (GET):

```csharp
public IActionResult Edit(int? id)
{
    if (id == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Please pass roll number to edit data";
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    var st = context.Students.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Roll == id);

    if (st == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Unable to find record for roll " + id;
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    return View(st);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Yahan 2 baar check kyu kar rahe hain?

**Student:**

### ✅ First Check – `id == null`

* Jab user **URL manually hit kare**
* Example: `/Home/Edit`
* Is case me **ID hi nahi aayi**

👉 Message show hoga:
**“Please pass roll number to edit data”**

---

### ✅ Second Check – `st == null`

* ID aayi hai
* Lekin **us roll ka record database me exist nahi karta**

👉 Message show hoga:
**“Unable to find record for roll X”**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar record mil gaya toh kya hoga?

**Student:**

* Student ka object **View ko pass** kar diya jayega
* View us object ke data se **form auto-fill** kar dega

---

# 🧱 Step 3: Edit View (Form)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit ka View kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Scaffolding se, bilkul Create ki tarah.

---

### 🧾 Steps:

* Edit action par **Right Click**
* **Add View**
* Template: **Edit**
* Model: **Student**
* DbContext: **TestDBContext**
* Click **Add**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form me Roll Number kyu nahi hota?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* **Roll Number = Primary Key**
* Primary Key **kabhi update nahi hoti**

Update hamesha **baaki fields** par hota hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form open hone par kya dikhta hai?

**Student:**

* Name, Gender, DOB, Phone
* **Pehle se bhara hua data**
* User sirf changes karta hai

---

# 🧱 Step 4: Edit – POST Action Method

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form submit karne par kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* Form submit hone par **POST request** jaati hai
* Same naam ka **Edit action (POST)** call hota hai
* Student ka updated object controller ko milta hai

---

### ✅ Controller Code (POST):

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Edit(Student st)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        context.Students.Update(st);
        context.SaveChanges();

        TempData["Success"] = "Data successfully updated";
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    return View(st);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: ModelState.IsValid yahan kyu check kar rahe hain?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* Validation rules (Required, Length) apply ho sakte hain
* Agar **phone digits galat** hain
* Ya koi required field missing hai

Toh:
👉 Form **wahi ka wahi wapas** aayega
👉 Error message ke saath

---

## ❓ Teacher: Update ke liye kaunsa method use hota hai?

**Student:**

```csharp
context.Students.Update(st);
context.SaveChanges();
```

* `Update()` → DbSet me change karta hai
* `SaveChanges()` → Database me update karta hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: INSERT aur UPDATE me main difference kya hai?

**Student:**

| Operation | Method     |
| --------- | ---------- |
| Insert    | `Add()`    |
| Update    | `Update()` |
| Delete    | `Remove()` |

---

---

**Student:**

1. List page open
2. Kisi student ke **Edit** pe click
3. Form open hua (data filled)

   * “Dilip Kumar” → “Dilip Kumar Sharma”

7. Message aaya:
   👉 **“Data successfully updated”**

**Student:**

* Update nahi hoga
* Form wapas aayega
* Error message dikhega
* Database safe rahega

# 📘 Part 37 – CRUD (DELETE Operation)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab tak hum CRUD me kaun-kaun se operations complete kar chuke hain?

Sir, ab tak humne:

* **CREATE (Insert)** ✔
* **READ (List & Details)** ✔
* **UPDATE (Edit)** ✔

Aaj **last operation – DELETE** padhenge.

---

## ❓ Teacher: DELETE operation ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
DELETE ka matlab hota hai:

👉 **Database se kisi record ko permanently remove karna**

Example:
Agar student ka record galat hai ya use hataana hai, toh hum **Delete** karte hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete button pe click karne par kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Delete button:

* **Delete action method** ko call karta hai
* Saath me **ID / Roll Number (Primary Key)** pass karta hai

Example URL:

```
/Home/Delete/4
```

Yahan **4 = Roll Number**

---

# 🧱 Step 1: DELETE – GET Action Method (Confirmation Page)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete karne ke liye sabse pehle kya banana padega?

**Student:**
Ek **Delete action method (GET)** banana padega, jo:

* Record find kare
* **Confirmation page** dikhaye

---

### ✅ Controller Code (GET):

```csharp
public IActionResult Delete(int? id)
{
    if (id == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Please pass roll number to delete data";
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    var st = context.Students.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Roll == id);

    if (st == null)
    {
        TempData["Message"] = "Unable to find record for roll " + id;
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    return View(st);
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Yahan pe 2 condition check kyu kar rahe hain?

**Student:**

### ✅ Case 1: `id == null`

* User ne URL manually hit kiya

  ```
  /Home/Delete
  ```
* ID pass hi nahi hui

👉 Message:
**“Please pass roll number to delete data”**

---

### ✅ Case 2: `st == null`

* ID aayi
* Lekin us ID ka record **database me exist nahi karta**

👉 Message:
**“Unable to find record for roll X”**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar record mil gaya toh kya hota hai?

**Student:**

* Student ka data **View ko pass** ho jaata hai
* View user se **confirmation** leta hai
  👉 “Are you sure you want to delete this?”

---

# 🧱 Step 2: DELETE View (Confirmation Page)

## ❓ Teacher: Delete ka View kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Scaffolding use karte hain.

* Delete action par **Right Click**
* **Add View**

* Model: **Student**
* DbContext: **TestDBContext**
* Click **Add**

**Student:**
Delete view me:

* Student ka **full detail (labels)** hota hai
* Message aata hai:
  👉 **“Are you sure you want to delete this?”**
* Do options:

  * **Delete (Confirm)**
  * **Back to List**

## ❓ Teacher: Delete View me form kyu hota hai?

# 🧱 Step 3: DELETE – POST Action Method (Actual Delete)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Actual delete kaha hota hai?

**Student:**
Actual delete hota hai:
👉 **POST Delete Action Method** me

---

### ✅ Controller Code (POST):

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Delete(Student st)
{
    if (st != null)
    {
        context.Students.Remove(st);
        context.SaveChanges();

        TempData["Success"] = "Data successfully deleted";
        return RedirectToAction("List");
    }

    TempData["Message"] = "Unable to delete data";
    return RedirectToAction("List");
}
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Yahan ModelState.IsValid kyu nahi lagaya?

**Student:**
Kyuki:

* Delete me **form fill nahi hota**
* Koi validation check karne wali cheez nahi hoti
* Sirf existing record delete hota hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete ke liye kaunsa EF Core method use hota hai?

**Student:**

| Operation | Method         |
| --------- | -------------- |
| Insert    | `Add()`        |
| Update    | `Update()`     |
| Delete    | **`Remove()`** |

---

## ❓ Teacher: Remove ke baad SaveChanges kyu zaroori hai?

**Student:**

* `Remove()` sirf **memory (DbSet)** se data hataata hai
* Database se permanently delete ke liye
  👉 **SaveChanges() compulsory hai**

---

   👉 **“Data successfully deleted”**

   * List se gayab
   * Database se bhi delete

**Student:**

* User ko error message milega
* Application crash nahi hogi
* User list page pe hi rahega

🎯 **Part 38: Understanding & Implementing Code First Approach in EF Core (ASP.NET Core MVC)**

📌 **Language:** Hindi-wali English (Hinglish)

# 📘 Part 38 – Code First Approach (Entity Framework Core)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Entity Framework me kaun-kaun se approaches hote hain?

**Student:**
Sir, Entity Framework me mainly **2 approaches** hoti hain:

1. **Database First**
2. **Code First**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database First aur Code First me basic difference kya hai?

**Student:**

### 🔹 **Database First**

* Pehle **database design** karte hain
* Phir **scaffolding** se:

  * **Model**
  * **DbContext** generate hota hai

### 🔹 **Code First**

* Pehle **Model classes** banate hain
* Phir **DbContext** banate hain
* Uske baad **database automatically generate** hota hai

👉 **CRUD operations dono me same hote hain**, sirf **implementation steps different** hote hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Code First approach exactly kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Code First Approach** ek development method hai jisme:

* Hum **.NET classes (models)** pehle banate hain
* Entity Framework un classes ke base par:

  * Database
  * Tables
  * Columns
    automatically generate kar deta hai

👉 Hume manually SQL likhne ki zarurat nahi hoti.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Code First kab use karna best hota hai?

**Student:**

Code First best hota hai jab:

* Aap **fresh application** bana rahe ho
* Pehle se koi **existing database nahi hai**
* Aapko **C# ka achha knowledge** hai
* Aap **domain-driven design** follow karna chahte ho

---

## ❓ Teacher: Domain ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Domain** ka matlab hota hai:

👉 Jis field ke liye application bana rahe ho

Examples:

* School app → Student, Teacher, Course
* Bank app → Account, Customer, Transaction

In sabke liye hum **domain models** banate hain.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Code First ke main advantages kya hain?

**Student:**

### ✅ Advantages:

* ❌ No visual database designer required
* ✅ Pure **code-based configuration**
* ✅ SQL aana zaroori nahi
* ✅ **Automatic database creation**
* ✅ Easy to maintain & modify
* ✅ Migration ke through **change tracking**

---

# 🧱 Overall Steps – Code First Approach

---

## ❓ Teacher: Code First ka complete flow batao

**Student:**

### 🔹 Step-by-step Flow:

1. **Create Project**
2. **Install required packages**
3. **Create Model class**
4. **Create DbContext class**
5. **Configure Connection String**
6. **Configure Program.cs**
7. **Create Migration**
8. **Run Migration (Update Database)**

---

# 🧩 Step 1: Required NuGet Packages

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kaun-kaun se packages install karne hote hain?

**Student:**

### 🔹 Mandatory 3 Packages:

1. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer**

   * Database provider (SQL Server)

2. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Tools**

   * Commands ke liye
   * (`Add-Migration`, `Update-Database`)

3. **Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design**

   * Migration & DbContext generation ke liye

👉 Ye **project-specific** packages hote hain, system-wide nahi.

---

# 🧩 Step 2: Create Model Class

---

## ❓ Teacher: Model class kya hoti hai?

**Student:**
Model class:

* Database table ko represent karti hai
* Har property = ek column

---

### 🧪 Example: Student Model

```csharp
public class Student
{
    public int SId { get; set; }
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public string Gender { get; set; }
    public DateOnly DOB { get; set; }
    public string Email { get; set; }
    public string Phone { get; set; }
}
```

👉 Iske base par **Student table** banegi.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar data type specify na karein toh kya hota hai?

**Student:**
EF Core automatically decide karta hai:

| C# Type  | SQL Type      |
| -------- | ------------- |
| int      | int           |
| string   | nvarchar(max) |
| DateOnly | date          |

---

# 🧩 Step 3: Create DbContext Class

---

## ❓ Teacher: DbContext ka role kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**DbContext** ek **bridge** hai:

👉 Application ↔ Database

Iska kaam:

* Database connection
* CRUD operations
* Change tracking
* Transactions

---

### 🧪 Example: DbContext Class

```csharp
public class StudentInfoDbContext : DbContext
{
    public StudentInfoDbContext(DbContextOptions<StudentInfoDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }

    public DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; }
}
```

👉 **Har model ke liye ek DbSet** hota hai.

---

# 🧩 Step 4: Configure Connection String

---

## ❓ Teacher: Connection string kahan likhte hain?

**Student:**
👉 `appsettings.json`

---

### 🧪 Example:

```json
"ConnectionStrings": {
  "ConStr": "Server=SERVER_NAME;
             Database=StudentInfo;
             Trusted_Connection=True;
             TrustServerCertificate=True"
}
```

---

# 🧩 Step 5: Configure Program.cs

---

## ❓ Teacher: Program.cs me kya configuration hota hai?

**Student:**
Yahan hum **DbContext ko register** karte hain.

---

### 🧪 Example:

```csharp
builder.Services.AddDbContext<StudentInfoDbContext>(
    options => options.UseSqlServer(
        builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("ConStr")
    )
);
```

👉 Ab application ko pata hai:

* Kaunsa DbContext
* Kaunsa database
* Kaunsi connection string

---

# 🧩 Step 6: Migration Concept

---

## ❓ Teacher: Migration kya hota hai?

**Student:**
**Migration** ek auto-generated C# class hoti hai jo:

* Model + DbContext ke base par
* Database creation / modification ka code rakhti hai

👉 Ye SQL ko directly generate aur execute karti hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Migration me kaun-kaun se methods hote hain?

**Student:**

| Method     | Kaam                                |
| ---------- | ----------------------------------- |
| **Up()**   | Database me changes apply karta hai |
| **Down()** | Changes rollback karta hai          |

---

## ❓ Teacher: Migration kaise banate hain?

**Student:**
Package Manager Console me:

```powershell
Add-Migration First
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Database kab create hota hai?

**Student:**
Jab hum migration **run** karte hain:

```powershell
Update-Database
```

👉 Isse:

* Database create hota hai
* Tables ban jaati hain

---

# 🧩 Step 7: Model Change → New Migration

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar model me change karein toh kya karna padega?

**Student:**

1. Model me change karo
2. **New Migration** banao
3. `Update-Database` run karo

---

### 🧪 Example: New Column Add

```csharp
public string Aadhaar { get; set; }
```

Commands:

```powershell
Add-Migration Third
Update-Database
```

👉 Sirf **difference ka code** generate hota hai, full table nahi.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Migration ka benefit kya hai?

**Student:**

* Database change history track hoti hai
* Old structure ka record rehta hai
* Team collaboration easy hota hai

## ❓ Teacher: Database ke saath kaam karne ke liye controller me sabse pehle kya chahiye?

### 🧪 DbContext Injection (Constructor)

```csharp
public class StudentController : Controller
{
```

# 📋 CRUD – Step by Step

# 🔹 1️⃣ LIST (Index)

---

## ❓ Teacher: List ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sir, **List** ka matlab database se **saare records fetch karke dikhana**.

---

## ❓ Teacher: List ke liye kaunsa action method banega?

**Student:**
Sir, **Index()** action method banega.

---

### 🧪 Index Action Method

```csharp
public IActionResult Index()
{
    var students = _context.Students.ToList();
    return View(students);
}
```

👉

* `_context.Students` → DbSet (table)
* `ToList()` → saara data laata hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: DbSet public kyun hona chahiye?

**Student:**
Sir, agar DbSet **public nahi hoga**, to controller me access nahi hoga.
Isliye hamesha:

```csharp
public DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; }
```

---

## ❓ Teacher: Index ka view kaise banate hain?

**Student:**

* Index action par Right click
* Add View → Razor View
* Template: **List**
* Model: **Student**

👉 Ye bilkul **Database First jaisa hi hota hai**.

## ❓ Teacher: Buttons ko link se button kaise banate hain?

```html
class="btn btn-primary text-white"

class="btn btn-danger text-white"
```

* **Edit → Primary**
* **Details → Warning**
* **Delete → Danger**

# ➕ 2️⃣ CREATE (Insert Data)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Data insert karne ke liye kya chahiye?

**Student:**
Sir, hume:

1. **GET Create** → Form dikhane ke liye
2. **POST Create** → Data save karne ke liye

---

### 🧪 GET: Create Action

```csharp
public IActionResult Create()
{
    return View();
}
```

---

### 🧪 Create View

* Template: **Create**
* Model: **Student**

👉 Ye automatically **form generate** kar deta hai.

---

## ❓ Teacher: Form submit hone ke baad kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sir, form submit hone par **POST Create** call hota hai.

---

### 🧪 POST: Create Action

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Create(Student student)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        _context.Students.Add(student);
        _context.SaveChanges();
        return RedirectToAction("Index");
    }
    return View(student);
}
```

👉

* **Add()** → DbSet me add
* **SaveChanges()** → Database me save
* **RedirectToAction("Index")** → List page par le jata hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Validation kaise lagate hain?

**Student:**
Sir, **Model class** me Data Annotations lagate hain.

---

### 🧪 Model Validation Example

```csharp
[Required]
public string Name { get; set; }

[EmailAddress]
public string Email { get; set; }

[Required]
public string Gender { get; set; }
```

👉

* **ModelState.IsValid** validation check karta hai
* Galat data pe error message show hota hai

---

## ❓ Teacher: Model me change karne ke baad kya bhoolna nahi chahiye?

**Student:**
Sir, **new Migration banana zaroori hai**:

```powershell
Add-Migration Fourth
Update-Database
```

# 🔍 3️⃣ DETAILS (Single Record)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Details ka matlab kya hota hai?

**Student:**
Sir, **Details** ka matlab ek **specific record ka full data dikhana**.

---

### 🧪 Details Action Method

```csharp
public IActionResult Details(int? id)
{
    if (id != null)
    {
        var student = _context.Students
            .FirstOrDefault(s => s.SId == id);

        if (student != null)
            return View(student);
    }
    return RedirectToAction("Index");
}
```

👉

* `FirstOrDefault()` → Record mile ya null
* `id == null` ya record na mile → Index page

---

### 🧪 Details View

* Template: **Details**
* Model: **Student**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Agar ID string hoti to comparison kaise karte?

**Student:**
Sir, string ke liye:

```csharp
item.Id.Equals(id)
```

👉 **Double equal (==)** sirf **int** ke liye safe hota hai.

---

# ✏️ 4️⃣ EDIT (Update Data)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit ke liye kitne action methods hote hain?

**Student:**
Sir, **2 action methods**:

1. **GET Edit** → Form show
2. **POST Edit** → Data update

---

### 🧪 GET: Edit Action

```csharp
public IActionResult Edit(int? id)
{
    if (id != null)
    {
        var student = _context.Students
            .FirstOrDefault(s => s.SId == id);

        if (student != null)
            return View(student);
    }
    return RedirectToAction("Index");
}
```

---

### 🧪 POST: Edit Action

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Edit(Student student)
{
    if (ModelState.IsValid)
    {
        _context.Students.Update(student);
        _context.SaveChanges();
        return RedirectToAction("Index");
    }
    return View(student);
}
```

👉

* **Update()** → Existing record update
* **SaveChanges()** → Database reflect

---

## ❓ Teacher: Edit form me data pehle se bhara hua kyun aata hai?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki hum **GET Edit me student object pass** karte hain, aur Razor automatically fields bind kar deta hai.

---

# 🗑️ 5️⃣ DELETE (Remove Data)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete ke liye flow kya hota hai?

**Student:**

1. **GET Delete** → Confirmation page
2. **POST Delete** → Actual delete

---

### 🧪 GET: Delete Action

```csharp
public IActionResult Delete(int? id)
{
    if (id != null)
    {
        var student = _context.Students
            .FirstOrDefault(s => s.SId == id);

        if (student != null)
            return View(student);
    }
    return RedirectToAction("Index");
}
```

---

### 🧪 POST: Delete Action

```csharp
[HttpPost]
public IActionResult Delete(Student student)
{
    if (student != null)
    {
        _context.Students.Remove(student);
        _context.SaveChanges();
    }
    return RedirectToAction("Index");
}
```

👉

* **Remove()** → Record delete
* **SaveChanges()** → Database update

---

## ❓ Teacher: Delete me ModelState check kyun nahi karte?

**Student:**
Sir, kyunki delete me **user koi data enter nahi karta**, sirf confirmation hota hai.

# 📘 Part 40 – Passing Multiple Models to View using **ViewModel**

---

## ❓ Teacher: Ab tak hum Entity Framework me kya-kya padh chuke hain?

**Student:**
Sir, ab tak humne ye padha:

* **Entity Framework Core**
* **Database First approach**
* **Code First approach**
* **CRUD operations**
* Ek time par **sirf ek model ka data** View me pass karna

---

## ❓ Teacher: Problem kya aati hai real-life applications me?

**Student:**
Sir, real-life me aksar:

* Ek **page par multiple tables ka data** dikhana hota hai
* Ya ek **form se data multiple tables me insert** hota hai

Example:

* Course ka detail + us course ke saare Students
* Student ka detail + uski Fee history

👉 Lekin MVC me **ek View sirf ek hi Model receive kar sakta hai** ❌

---

## ❓ Teacher: To multiple tables ka data ek View me kaise bhejte hain?

**Student:**
Sir, iske liye hum **ViewModel** use karte hain ✅

---

# 🔑 Important Rule (Yaad rakhna)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Kya hum directly 2 models View me bhej sakte hain?

**Student:**
❌ **Nahi sir**

* Ek action method → **sirf ek model return** kar sakta hai
* Ek view → **sirf ek model accept** karta hai

👉 Isliye hume **custom class banana padta hai**
👉 Isi class ko bolte hain **ViewModel**

---

# 🧠 ViewModel kya hota hai?

---

## ❓ Teacher: ViewModel kya hota hai simple words me?

**Student:**
**ViewModel** ek **special class** hoti hai jo:

* **Multiple models ka data ek jagah collect karti hai**
* Sirf **View ke requirement ke liye design hoti hai**
* Direct database table se linked nahi hoti

👉 Isme hum:

* Ek model ka **single object**
* Dusre model ki **List**
  rakh sakte hain

---

# 🗄️ Example Scenario (Video ka main example)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Video me kaunsa real-life scenario liya gaya hai?

**Student:**

### 🎓 Example:

* **Course table**
* **Student table**

Relation:

* **One Course → Many Students**
* Student table me sirf **CourseId (FK)** hota hai

---

### Requirement:

1. User **Course ID enter kare**
2. Page par:

   * Upar → **Course details**
   * Neeche → **Us course ke saare students**

👉 Yahan **2 tables ka data ek hi view me chahiye**

# 🧱 Step 1: Database Design (DB First)

---

## ❓ Teacher: Pehle kya banate hain?
